# Competitor research: AI sales and GTM platforms

Prepared for Demand AI (Next Best Campaign). Research date: 27 October 2026.

This document sets out what the leading AI-native sales platforms and the established incumbents do to deliver results, how they generate their outputs, why they get the results they get, what sets each of them apart, and what Demand AI can learn and adopt.

## How to read this document

- **Scope.** 28 entries: 20 AI-native platforms (Group A, including Nooks) and 8 established players (Group B). Entries 4 and 16 are the same product (Leadsforge), and Baze could not be identified, so 26 companies are researched in full plus one identification note.
- **Each company section** opens with a **Differentiator** (what is genuinely different about their approach) and a **Source coverage** line. The coverage line shows, for each of the 12 source types (site, pricing, docs, changelog, blog, case studies, video, leadership, press, jobs, integrations, filings), whether it was read in full, seen only as a search extract, not found, or could not be accessed. The seven headings follow.
- **Citations.** Every claim carries an inline link to the page it came from. Third-party coverage is used only to verify or add context and is marked "(third-party)". "(search extract)" means the page was seen through a search result rather than opened in full.
- **Labels.** "Vendor claim" marks a figure a company states about itself that we could not verify. "Not published" means the company does not disclose the detail. "Inference:" marks our own reasoning, not fact.
- **Access limits.** The research environment's network policy blocks direct page fetches from vendor sites, and the scraping tool was rate-limited. Most first-party pages were still read in full through the scraping tool, but some (often YouTube, podcasts, LinkedIn and a few help centres) were reached only through search extracts. The source coverage line for each company shows exactly where.
- **"What I liked about their approach"** is left blank in every section for you to complete.
- **Stages.** Each "What we can learn and adopt" bullet starts with the Demand AI stage it applies to: Prospecting, Qualified lead identification, Lead scoring, Segmentation, NBA and NBC, Campaign execution, Content creation, Content mapping, Follow-up and response tracking, Deal.

## Group A: AI-native platforms

### 1. Clarify (with Seam AI)
**Website:** [clarify.ai](https://www.clarify.ai) and [getseam.ai](https://www.getseam.ai/) | **Founded / HQ:** Clarify early 2024, Seattle, with a San Francisco office since the Seam deal; Seam 2020, San Francisco ([GeekWire, third-party](https://www.geekwire.com/2026/seattles-clarify-acquires-s-f-startup-seam-ai-joining-forces-to-challenge-crm-stalwarts/)) | **Category:** AI-native "autonomous" CRM, adding market signals and account-based marketing (ABM) through the Seam acquisition

**Differentiator:** Clarify builds the CRM itself from inbox, calendar and call capture, then lets users create prompt-defined "AI fields" and permissioned agents on top of that record. Seam adds the outside-in half: ICP fit models written as natural-language questions, back-tested on known good and bad accounts, plus 30+ trigger-based plays. The combination puts fit, signals and next action inside the system of record, rather than in a separate intent tool.

**Source coverage:** Website: read ([home](https://www.clarify.ai), [agents](https://www.clarify.ai/agents), [signals](https://www.clarify.ai/signals)) | Pricing: read in pass 1 ([pricing](https://www.clarify.ai/pricing)) | Docs: read ([AI fields](https://docs.clarify.ai/en/articles/12407767-ai-fields); Seam [overview](https://docs.getseam.ai/onboarding/getting-started/overview), [ICP fit](https://docs.getseam.ai/onboarding/configure/configuring-icp-fit.md), [signals](https://docs.getseam.ai/onboarding/configure/configuring-signals.md), [plays](https://docs.getseam.ai/onboarding/orchestrate/plays.md)) | Changelog: not found as a first-party page; third-party tracker search extract only ([Industry Lens](https://industry-lens.com/reports/signal-spotlight-clarify/md), [PricingSaaS](https://pricingsaas.com/companies/clarify/diffs/2026W27)) | Blog: read ([acquisition post](https://www.clarify.ai/blog/welcoming-seam-ai-to-clarify)) | Case studies: read ([FutureFit AI](https://www.clarify.ai/customers/futurefit-ai), [Tesorio](https://www.clarify.ai/customers/tesorio)) | Video: search extract only (customer videos embedded on the [FutureFit page](https://www.clarify.ai/customers/futurefit-ai); YouTube channel has 133 subscribers) | Founder content: search extract only ([Village Global podcast](https://www.castfox.net/podcast/village-global-podcast-61102/episode/recall-sessions-ai-native-crms-and-what-it-takes-to-replace-salesforce-doug-camplejohn-and-patrick-thompson-45674), [Substack interview](https://aspiringforintelligence.substack.com/p/a-conversation-with-clarifys-co-founders)) | Funding and press: read ([GeekWire](https://www.geekwire.com/2026/seattles-clarify-acquires-s-f-startup-seam-ai-joining-forces-to-challenge-crm-stalwarts/), [Series A post](https://www.clarify.ai/blog/series-a)) | Jobs: read, 2 postings ([Ashby board](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/clarify), [Product Engineer](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/clarify/11c08a04-3ab0-4fc4-964e-88ff292d279e)) | Integrations: read ([home](https://www.clarify.ai), [AWS Marketplace, third-party](https://aws.amazon.com/marketplace/pp/prodview-vkrkvx5hvhabc)) | Investor filings: not applicable

#### 1. Why they are performing well
**Verified facts**
- Clarify raised a $15m Series A in June 2025 (total $22.5m) from U.S. Venture Partners, Gradient Ventures and Madrona ([Clarify blog](https://www.clarify.ai/blog/series-a); [GeekWire, third-party](https://www.geekwire.com/2026/seattles-clarify-acquires-s-f-startup-seam-ai-joining-forces-to-challenge-crm-stalwarts/)). The founders previously sold Iteratively to Amplitude.
- **The Seam deal, 21 July 2026:** Clarify's first acquisition, terms undisclosed. Five Seam staff joined, including CEO Nicholas Scavone. Clarify raised extra undisclosed money alongside and is considering a Series B in early 2027 ([GeekWire, third-party](https://www.geekwire.com/2026/seattles-clarify-acquires-s-f-startup-seam-ai-joining-forces-to-challenge-crm-stalwarts/)). The same day Clarify announced "Clarify Signals", now on a waitlist with early access "over the coming months" ([blog](https://www.clarify.ai/blog/welcoming-seam-ai-to-clarify); [signals page](https://www.clarify.ai/signals)).
- **What Seam brings:** about $7m raised (Bessemer-led seed) and customers including Zapier, GoFundMe, Drata and Betterment ([blog](https://www.clarify.ai/blog/welcoming-seam-ai-to-clarify); [GeekWire, third-party](https://www.geekwire.com/2026/seattles-clarify-acquires-s-f-startup-seam-ai-joining-forces-to-challenge-crm-stalwarts/)). Seam's homepage now says it is "joining Clarify to build the CRM that generates revenue, not just manages it" ([getseam.ai](https://www.getseam.ai/)).
- Headcount is small: the current job ad says "a team of ~25 people" and "self-serve from early traction to a repeatable, growing revenue channel" is the goal, which shows product-led growth is the current investment ([Product Engineer posting](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/clarify/11c08a04-3ab0-4fc4-964e-88ff292d279e)). Only two roles are open, both engineering.
- Named case studies with a long-cycle customer: FutureFit AI sells to state agencies and employers on "12 to 18 month" cycles, with "nine sub-segments across two ICPs" ([FutureFit](https://www.clarify.ai/customers/futurefit-ai)).
- Third-party trackers report Agents reached general availability on 23 June 2026, the external API went public on 24 July and "Custom Agents" was added to all plans ([Industry Lens, third-party](https://industry-lens.com/reports/signal-spotlight-clarify/md); [PricingSaaS, third-party](https://pricingsaas.com/companies/clarify/diffs/2026W27)) (search extract).

**Vendor claims**
- "Trusted by 8,000+ GTM teams" ([home](https://www.clarify.ai)).
- Tesorio: "$50,000+/yr in tools cut", about five hours saved per deal, Q1 pipeline goal beaten by 34%. Defensive Networks: "98% less CRM data entry", about 120 hours a week removed. FutureFit: meeting prep from 15 to about 5 minutes ([home](https://www.clarify.ai); [FutureFit](https://www.clarify.ai/customers/futurefit-ai)).
- Seam Agent Plays: "4x faster signal-to-outreach" and "35% increase in qualified pipeline" for early adopters ([Seam blog](https://www.getseam.ai/blog/introducing-seam-s-agent-plays)).

#### 2. How they promise results
- Clarify sells removal of admin and tool consolidation ("Selling is human. Clarify runs the rest"), and every customer story leads with tools cancelled and hours saved ([home](https://www.clarify.ai)).
- **Priced on AI work done, not seats.** Unlimited seats on every plan; credits pay for AI actions; enrichment and recording are free ([pricing](https://www.clarify.ai/pricing)). The docs make the unit economics explicit: an AI field costs 3 credits per record, or 30 if internet research is switched on, and only successful fills are charged ([AI fields docs](https://docs.clarify.ai/en/articles/12407767-ai-fields)).
- Seam framed results as pipeline from acting on "intent spikes", and its docs tell customers to validate a play on 10 to 20 accounts for one to two weeks before scaling ([Seam plays docs](https://docs.getseam.ai/onboarding/orchestrate/plays.md)).

#### 3. How they generate their outputs
1. **Capture:** connect email and calendar; Clarify "reads the conversations you've already had and builds your contacts, companies, and deals, history included". Calls are recorded "with or without a bot", summarised and written to the record ([home](https://www.clarify.ai)).
2. **Prospect:** Lead Finder turns a plain-English request into a saved search over a built-in database of "28 million companies and over 175 million people", with an "email score" per contact ([agents FAQ](https://www.clarify.ai/agents); [home](https://www.clarify.ai)).
3. **Score and segment with AI fields:** the user writes instructions (for example "Return 'Strong fit' if all criteria match"), optionally adds related records as context ({People > Meetings}) and optionally enables web research. The model writes the value directly to the field; there is no review step, but "AI reasoning" shows the logic and sources ([AI fields docs](https://docs.clarify.ai/en/articles/12407767-ai-fields)). FutureFit used this to classify inbound leads by target market: "scarily accurate" ([FutureFit](https://www.clarify.ai/customers/futurefit-ai)).
4. **Seam fit scoring (to be folded in):** ICP criteria written as natural-language questions ("Do they underwrite merchants?", "Is this company a direct competitor?"), several fit models per segment or product line, a 45-account back-test (15 known good, 15 known bad, 15 random), A to D grades with a written explanation synced to a CRM custom object ([ICP fit docs](https://docs.getseam.ai/onboarding/configure/configuring-icp-fit.md)).
5. **Seam signals and plays:** persona signals (job change, new hire, job opening, promotion) and keyword signals (social listening, funding, social engagement) feed plays. A play is: audience trigger, optional qualifier, prospecting rule (3 to 5 contacts per account, chosen personas, email and mobile enrichment), routing, then deployment to CRM, Outreach, Salesloft or Apollo, LinkedIn or Google audiences, or Marketo ([signals](https://docs.getseam.ai/onboarding/configure/configuring-signals.md); [plays](https://docs.getseam.ai/onboarding/orchestrate/plays.md)).
6. **Execute:** the agent drafts a multi-step campaign (LinkedIn connection note, email two days later, threaded follow-up three days later) for review before sending, and drafts replies "in your voice" ([home](https://www.clarify.ai)).
7. **Follow up and report:** post-meeting agents update stage and next steps and draft a follow-up grounded in the transcript; reports attribute pipeline to the campaign that sourced it ([agents](https://www.clarify.ai/agents); [home](https://www.clarify.ai)).

#### 4. Technical mechanisms and interesting approaches
- **One record graph, inside plus outside.** Clarify "understands everything happening inside your business"; Seam "understood everything happening outside it". Signals are meant to become native CRM context that agents read ([blog](https://www.clarify.ai/blog/welcoming-seam-ai-to-clarify)).
- **Agents as permissioned teammates.** Each agent (the assistant is called "Rep") has a trigger, instructions and scoped rights: can read, can write, can reach (Slack, Gmail, MCP servers), can learn "from your edits and feedback". Every action is logged. Skills are reusable plain-language instructions ([agents](https://www.clarify.ai/agents)). A team comment on Product Hunt says write tools can require approval per tool, per agent ([Product Hunt](https://www.producthunt.com/products/clarify-6)) (search extract).
- **Bounded context by design.** AI fields read only the 10 most recent related records, use meeting summaries not transcripts, and strip quoted email history ([AI fields docs](https://docs.clarify.ai/en/articles/12407767-ai-fields)). This is a published cost and quality trade-off.
- **Back-tested fit models (Seam).** The 45-account validation set is a simple, published evaluation loop for an LLM scorer ([ICP fit docs](https://docs.getseam.ai/onboarding/configure/configuring-icp-fit.md)).
- **Novel signal: AI search visitors.** A Seam play identifies "companies discovering your solution through AI search platforms like Claude and ChatGPT" ([plays](https://docs.getseam.ai/onboarding/orchestrate/plays.md)). Mechanism: Not published.
- **MCP-first distribution:** an official MCP server listed in the Claude, ChatGPT, Cursor and Slack marketplaces, plus iMessage and email in early access ([home](https://www.clarify.ai)).
- Models, identity resolution behind website visitor plays, and how signals will be weighted inside Clarify: Not published. Jobs reveal no AI stack detail ([Product Engineer posting](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/clarify/11c08a04-3ab0-4fc4-964e-88ff292d279e)).

#### 5. Why they get the results they get
- **Workflow fit:** the CRM fills itself from inbox and calls, which removes the main reason small teams abandon CRMs (FutureFit's HubSpot "sat unused") ([FutureFit](https://www.clarify.ai/customers/futurefit-ai)).
- **Consolidation economics plus seat-free pricing** make switching a cost saving, not a new line item.
- **Fit to long cycles:** daily briefs and Ask Deals summarise months of history; FutureFit notes that proactive surfacing of stalled deals "remains aspirational today", which is an honest limit ([FutureFit](https://www.clarify.ai/customers/futurefit-ai)).
- **Signals as a moat:** Seam's data and ABM customer base move Clarify toward 6sense and Demandbase territory.
- Inference: the 8,000+ teams figure is mostly free and small-team usage; ARR is not published.

#### 6. What we can learn and adopt
- **Lead scoring:** copy Seam's back-test. Before a client trusts our tiers, score 15 known wins, 15 known losses and 15 random accounts and show whether wins land in tier A or B. This is cheap to build in a rules-based prototype.
- **Lead scoring:** add LLM "fit questions" written in plain language (for example "Does this plant run continuous process lines?") alongside rules, each returning a grade plus a one-paragraph explanation stored on the account.
- **Segmentation:** support several fit models per product line or segment, as Seam does ("Manufacturing fit score", "Enterprise fit score").
- **Qualified lead identification:** show the cost before running enrichment or research, and offer "run on first 10 rows" as Clarify does.
- **NBA and NBC:** model each NBC as a Seam-style play: trigger audience, qualifier, personas and contacts per account (3 to 5 for buying committees), channel destination. Add "multi-threading" (only one contact engaged) and "high intent, no sales activity" as standard plays.
- **Follow-up and response tracking:** track pipeline sourced per play or campaign, as Clarify's dinner report does, so NBC impact can be proven.

#### 7. What I liked about their approach
_To be completed._

### 2. Reevo
**Website:** [reevo.ai](https://reevo.ai) | **Founded / HQ:** 2024, Santa Clara, California, with a San Francisco office ([Ciro release](https://www.wboc.com/online_features/press_releases/reevo-strengthens-its-ai-powered-prospecting-and-outreach-with-acquisition-of-ciro/article_40518000-e84f-5e94-874d-38c3c0746d74.html); [jobs](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/reevo)) | **Category:** AI-native revenue operating system (CRM, prospecting, sequencing, dialler, meeting intelligence, forecasting)

**Differentiator:** Reevo is betting capital on owning the whole stack and the first-party activity data it produces, so that agents can track "every signal on a deal as it arrives" and hand reps a drafted action to approve. Its job ads show it is investing in the hard infrastructure (memory, evaluation, durable execution, reinforcement learning) rather than prompt wrappers. Prospecting intelligence is being bought (Ciro) rather than built, and intent and lookalike scoring are still unreleased.

**Source coverage:** Website: read ([Find](https://reevo.ai/products/find), [Foundation](https://reevo.ai/products/foundation), [home](https://reevo.ai)) | Pricing: not published; third-party only ([Salesforge review, third-party competitor](https://www.salesforge.ai/blog/reevo-ai-review); [PricingSaaS, third-party](https://pricingsaas.com/companies/reevo/diffs/2026W34), search extract) | Docs: not found | Changelog: not found as a first-party page; third-party tracker search extract only ([PricingSaaS](https://pricingsaas.com/companies/reevo/diffs/2026W34)) | Blog: not found | Case studies: not found (testimonials only on [home](https://reevo.ai)) | Video: not found (product tour page exists at [reevo.ai/product-tour](https://reevo.ai/product-tour), not read) | Founder content: search extract only ([GTMnow podcast with David Zhu](https://gtmnow.com/gtm-190-ai-native-gtm-strategy-reevo-david-zhu/)) | Funding and press: read ([Business Wire launch](https://businesswire.com/news/home/20251105759977/en/Reevo-Debuts-with-$80-Million-in-Funding-as-the-Only-AI-Native-GTM-Platform-Spanning-Marketing-Sales-and-Customer-Success), [exec hires](https://businesswire.com/news/home/20260226926076/en/Revenue-Marketing-Builders-Join-Reevo-to-Deliver-AI-Native-Revenue-Operating-System-to-Customers), [Ciro release](https://www.wboc.com/online_features/press_releases/reevo-strengthens-its-ai-powered-prospecting-and-outreach-with-acquisition-of-ciro/article_40518000-e84f-5e94-874d-38c3c0746d74.html), [Kleiner Perkins](https://www.kleinerperkins.com/perspectives/reevo-the-intelligent-revenue-operating-system/)) | Jobs: read, 21 open, 2 read in full ([board](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/reevo), [Senior SWE, AI](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/reevo/4b687cbc-db80-4ebd-93b9-f07fdd4b802f), [Product Lead, Find](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/reevo/6c5017f6-f43a-4332-a414-4cab85201e13)) | Integrations: read ([Foundation](https://reevo.ai/products/foundation)) | Investor filings: not applicable

#### 1. Why they are performing well
**Verified facts**
- Launched 5 November 2025 with $80m, co-led by Khosla Ventures and Kleiner Perkins ([Business Wire](https://businesswire.com/news/home/20251105759977/en/Reevo-Debuts-with-$80-Million-in-Funding-as-the-Only-AI-Native-GTM-Platform-Spanning-Marketing-Sales-and-Customer-Success)). Third-party coverage splits this into a $10m seed and $70m Series A at about a $500m valuation; the valuation has no primary source ([SaaStr, third-party](https://www.saastr.com/saastr-ai-app-of-the-week-reevo-the-500m-bet-that-your-entire-gtm-stack-should-be-one-platform)).
- Senior hires: CMO Naman Khan (ex-Salesforce, Dropbox) and CRO Ali Ghotbi (14 years at Box) ([Business Wire](https://businesswire.com/news/home/20260226926076/en/Revenue-Marketing-Builders-Join-Reevo-to-Deliver-AI-Native-Revenue-Operating-System-to-Customers)).
- 8 July 2026: acquired Ciro, a YC-backed prospecting agent "used by hundreds of sales teams" ([Ciro release](https://www.wboc.com/online_features/press_releases/reevo-strengthens-its-ai-powered-prospecting-and-outreach-with-acquisition-of-ciro/article_40518000-e84f-5e94-874d-38c3c0746d74.html)).
- **Hiring now:** 21 open roles across sales, marketing, product, engineering and customer success, mostly on-site. Three are "Forward Deployed" roles (Generalist, Revenue Operations), and the AI job ad says "Our team of 100+ builders has 30 exits under their belt" ([jobs board](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/reevo); [AI posting](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/reevo/4b687cbc-db80-4ebd-93b9-f07fdd4b802f)).
- Named testimonials from CodeCargo, Propel and Casca ([Find](https://reevo.ai/products/find); [home](https://reevo.ai)).

**Vendor claims**
- "4x growth since its November 2025 launch", baseline not stated ([Ciro release](https://www.wboc.com/online_features/press_releases/reevo-strengthens-its-ai-powered-prospecting-and-outreach-with-acquisition-of-ciro/article_40518000-e84f-5e94-874d-38c3c0746d74.html)).
- "Every rep a day back each week" ([home](https://reevo.ai)). ARR, customer count and retention are not disclosed ([Future Nexus, third-party](https://www.heyfuturenexus.com/posts/funded-reevo-lands-80m-seed-to-unify-gtm-chaos-into-one-ai-native-system)).

#### 2. How they promise results
- "Revenue should feel predictable", by replacing the "four or five separate tools" teams pay for. The Find product lead ad quantifies the pain the company is selling against: reps spend "2-4 hours / week" updating CRM, and sales orgs run "10 (sales org of ~25 people) to 20+" point tools ([Product Lead, Find](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/reevo/6c5017f6-f43a-4332-a414-4cab85201e13)).
- Outcomes on the site are qualitative. No quantified case studies.
- Pricing: quote-based, reportedly Core, Pro and Enterprise ([Capterra, third-party](https://www.capterra.co.uk/software/1172503/Reevo)). A pricing tracker reports Reevo Connect plans dropped the email quota line and domain and inbox limits in August 2026 ([PricingSaaS, third-party](https://pricingsaas.com/companies/reevo/diffs/2026W34)) (search extract).

#### 3. How they generate their outputs
1. **Data core:** Reevo generates its own first-party activity data from emails, meetings and calendars, rather than syncing from other tools ([Business Wire](https://businesswire.com/news/home/20251105759977/en/Reevo-Debuts-with-$80-Million-in-Funding-as-the-Only-AI-Native-GTM-Platform-Spanning-Marketing-Sales-and-Customer-Success)).
2. **Find:** TAM sourcing from a built-in database of "millions of verified contacts"; one-click enrichment with "every email verified against the prospect's company"; live web research saved to the record "with sources"; new contacts "validated, deduped, and mapped against existing records"; web-form leads qualified "against your screening criteria"; rule-based routing by territory, size and availability ([Find](https://reevo.ai/products/find)).
3. **Ciro search (now native):** lists built with "more than ten qualifying criteria per contact", with remembered ICP, exclusion rules and voice, over a multi-terabyte index refined across "multiple search passes" ([Ciro release](https://www.wboc.com/online_features/press_releases/reevo-strengthens-its-ai-powered-prospecting-and-outreach-with-acquisition-of-ciro/article_40518000-e84f-5e94-874d-38c3c0746d74.html)).
4. **Connect:** domain purchase, warming, sequences, dialler ([home](https://reevo.ai)).
5. **Next action:** a home feed of "what changed overnight, ranked by what matters"; each proposed action can be approved, edited or dismissed; "hand over more as trust grows" ([Foundation](https://reevo.ai/products/foundation)). The AI team "track[s] every signal on a deal as it arrives, work[s] out the next move, and hand[s] reps a drafted action to approve" ([AI posting](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/reevo/4b687cbc-db80-4ebd-93b9-f07fdd4b802f)).
6. **Ask Reevo:** answers about pipeline that cite "the exact call, email, or record", plus deep research, scheduled prompts and Slack mentions ([Foundation](https://reevo.ai/products/foundation)).
7. **Not yet shipped:** intent signals (company news, competitor content engagement, technology shifts, hiring) and "signal prospecting" where "every win triggers a search for lookalike buyers, scored by fit" are both marked "Soon" ([Find](https://reevo.ai/products/find)).

#### 4. Technical mechanisms and interesting approaches
- **Agent infrastructure in the job ad:** memory and retrieval (RAG) "at enterprise data volume"; evaluation systems "reliable enough to gate a launch on"; "fine-tuning to reinforcement learning" with "tool and agent interfaces clean enough to RL against"; durable execution and "Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) checkpoints" for long-running workflows; sandboxed agentic code execution; role-adaptive AI interfaces ([AI posting](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/reevo/4b687cbc-db80-4ebd-93b9-f07fdd4b802f)). Inference: Reevo plans to train its own models on its first-party activity data.
- **Find roadmap in the product ad:** enrichment waterfalls with "provider selection, waterfall logic, match quality, coverage benchmarks, and cost-per-record economics"; entity resolution; "which signals matter per segment, how they're scored, and how they trigger downstream action"; ICP and lookalike modelling that "turns a customer's closed-won history into a ranked universe of next-best accounts"; discovery UX that shows "why this account, why now" ([Product Lead, Find](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/reevo/6c5017f6-f43a-4332-a414-4cab85201e13)).
- **Iterative retrieval with preference memory** (Ciro), and a closed loop where "everything learned downstream feeds back into who our customers go after next" ([Ciro release](https://www.wboc.com/online_features/press_releases/reevo-strengthens-its-ai-powered-prospecting-and-outreach-with-acquisition-of-ciro/article_40518000-e84f-5e94-874d-38c3c0746d74.html)). Mechanism: Not published.
- **Governance:** workflow dry-runs, field change history, automated stage gating ([Foundation](https://reevo.ai/products/foundation)).
- MCP into Notion, Linear, Pylon and PostHog; integrations with Salesforce, HubSpot, Zoom, Granola, Teams ([Foundation](https://reevo.ai/products/foundation)).
- Models, scoring method and forecasting approach: Not published.

#### 5. Why they get the results they get
- **Capital buys breadth:** $80m funds a full suite, senior hires and acquisitions.
- **Forward-deployed onboarding:** Inference: three forward-deployed roles mean results depend partly on Reevo staff configuring each customer.
- **Wedge:** small and mid-market teams leaving HubSpot, sold on consolidation.
- Inference: traction today comes from the funding story and consolidation pitch more than published outcomes; the signal and lookalike engine that would differentiate Find is still being built.

#### 6. What we can learn and adopt
- **Segmentation:** build "closed-won lookalikes" as an automatic micro-segment: take the client's won accounts and rank the next most similar accounts. Reevo is hiring to do this; we can start with rules on industry, size and product line.
- **Lead scoring:** score signals per segment, as Reevo's roadmap describes ("which signals matter per segment"). A plant expansion may matter for one product line and not another.
- **NBA and NBC:** show a ranked "what changed since last time" feed with approve, edit or dismiss per action, and record acceptance rate to decide when to automate more.
- **Qualified lead identification:** verify each contact against the account's domain and map to existing accounts before import, extending our de-duplication.
- **NBA and NBC:** for every recommended account, display "why this account, why now" with a link to the evidence.
- **Follow-up and response tracking:** log outcomes back against the segment and play, so targeting learns from replies and meetings.

### 3. nRev
**Website:** [nrev.ai](https://www.nrev.ai) | **Founded / HQ:** 2022 as Nurturev, by Sayanta Ghosh, Nikhil Ojha and Rajat Jain; Pune, India ([Venture Intelligence, third-party](https://news.ventureintelligence.com/private-equity/revenue-intelligence-player-nurturev-raises-$1-m-led-by-antler-india)) | **Category:** GTM automation platform ("AI GTM engineer"): signal-based workflows and playbooks for RevOps

**Differentiator:** nRev sells packaged, deterministic GTM workflows: an AI builder plans the node graph, but runs follow fixed paths with per-node credit estimates, free test runs, logging and undo. Its signal library includes unusual sources such as competitor sales reps' LinkedIn activity and Google Jobs scraping, and top tiers bundle human GTM-engineer hours, so it sits between a tool and a service.

**Source coverage:** Website: read ([home](https://www.nrev.ai)) | Pricing: read ([home pricing section](https://www.nrev.ai)) | Docs: read ([overview](https://docs.nrev.ai/), [AI Builder](https://docs.nrev.ai/building_workflows/ai_builder.md), [AI Scorer](https://docs.nrev.ai/nodes/ai_toolkit/ai_scorer_node.md)) | Changelog: not found | Blog: not found (playbook pages linked from [home](https://www.nrev.ai) not read) | Case studies: read ([Mysa](https://www.nrev.ai/customer-stories/how-mysa-io-built-an-ai-powered-intent-engine-for-pipeline-growth), [Mindtickle](https://www.nrev.ai/customer-stories/mindtickle-fully-automated-pipeline-generation-inside-salesforce), [PriceLabs](https://www.nrev.ai/customer-stories/how-pricelabs-built-an-ai-powered-outbound-engine)) | Video: search extract only ([nRev, Your AI GTM Engineer](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VpOUecUq7aU); channel has 32 subscribers) | Founder content: search extract only ([Product Hunt launch, via hunted.space](https://hunted.space/product/nrev)) | Funding and press: search extract only ([Venture Intelligence](https://news.ventureintelligence.com/private-equity/revenue-intelligence-player-nurturev-raises-$1-m-led-by-antler-india), [Inc42](https://inc42.com/company/nurturev/funding/)) | Jobs: read, 1 posting, now closed ([Antler board](https://careers.antler.co/companies/nurturev-2/jobs/55625161-gtm-engineer)) | Integrations: search extract only ([integrations page](https://www.nrev.ai/integrations) not read; list on [home](https://www.nrev.ai)) | Investor filings: not applicable

#### 1. Why they are performing well
**Verified facts**
- Funding: $1m pre-seed in early 2024 led by Antler India, with Sparrow Capital, ISV Capital, Blume Founders Fund and angels Kunal Shah and Deepak Diwakar ([Venture Intelligence, third-party](https://news.ventureintelligence.com/private-equity/revenue-intelligence-player-nurturev-raises-$1-m-led-by-antler-india)). A further $1.2m seed from Xeed Ventures in February 2025 is listed by one database but not confirmed by press ([Inc42, third-party](https://inc42.com/company/nurturev/funding/)) (search extract). Pass 1 said "Funding: Not published"; this is corrected.
- Five named customer stories on the site: Mindtickle, PriceLabs, Mysa, Oloid and SentiSum ([home](https://www.nrev.ai)).
- SOC 2 Type II, AES-256 at rest, TLS 1.3 in transit ([home](https://www.nrev.ai)).
- Product Hunt: 80 upvotes, 54 comments, #77 on the day ([hunted.space, third-party](https://hunted.space/product/nrev)). YouTube channel: 32 subscribers ([home embed](https://www.nrev.ai)). Inference: brand reach is small.

**Vendor claims**
- "Trained on 10,000+ live sales & marketing engines" ([home](https://www.nrev.ai)).
- Mindtickle "~400/wk qualified ICP leads into CRM"; PriceLabs 500 prospects a week enriched; Oloid "$10K saved upfront" and "50+/wk high-confidence opportunities"; SentiSum "15/day priority profiles actioned per sender" ([home](https://www.nrev.ai)).
- Replaces a stack costing "~$7,036/mo" for $999 a month ([home](https://www.nrev.ai)).

#### 2. How they promise results
- Tool replacement plus pipeline automation. A table names what each capability replaces (UserGems, Regie, Common Room, Unify, RB2B, HeyReach, Instantly, Clay, Apollo, ZoomInfo, n8n) with prices ([home](https://www.nrev.ai)).
- Usage-based credits: Nudge $149 (15k credits), Growth $399 (40k), Scale $999 (110k plus 5 hours of GTM support a month), Done for you $2,499 (300k plus 20 hours). 2,500 free credits; Test Mode is free; credits show above each node; published plays are free to copy ([home](https://www.nrev.ai)). An ROI calculator exists ([calculator](https://www.nrev.ai/pricing-calculator)).
- Each case study ends "Want us to implement this for you?" with a founder calendar link ([Mysa](https://www.nrev.ai/customer-stories/how-mysa-io-built-an-ai-powered-intent-engine-for-pipeline-growth)). Inference: outcomes are partly delivered as a service.

#### 3. How they generate their outputs
The Mysa case gives the clearest end-to-end process ([Mysa](https://www.nrev.ai/customer-stories/how-mysa-io-built-an-ai-powered-intent-engine-for-pipeline-growth)):
1. **Listen (weekly):** three listeners: Google Jobs scraping for ICP-relevant roles, website visitor identification, and competitor sales reps' LinkedIn engagement with ICP companies.
2. **Qualify:** "AI filters and qualifies companies against Mysa's ICP, producing a curated, prioritized account list."
3. **Research, phase 1:** company announcements from the last six months, job posts, tech stack, LinkedIn company posts, decision-makers, contact-level LinkedIn activity, and sentiment across reviews and forums.
4. **Research, phase 2:** AI extracts structured business events: expansion, key hires, funding, launches, operational shifts.
5. **Score, phase 3:** "a normalized 0 to 100 score based on weighted buying signals", where funding and team scaling outweigh passive signals, and "each score includes a clear rationale".
6. **Generate:** LinkedIn connection notes, LinkedIn follow-ups, multi-step email sequences and contact-specific talking points "tied to real events".
7. **Send with safeguards:** daily caps, one message at a time per contact, warm-up actions, automatic pause on reply, AI reply detection, Slack alert for human takeover.
8. **On demand:** drop a domain into Slack ("Slack Genie") and get events, hiring, stack, decision-makers, a 0 to 100 score and talking points within minutes.

Other observed patterns: Mindtickle leads created and routed in Salesforce with "zero human intervention" ([Mindtickle](https://www.nrev.ai/customer-stories/mindtickle-fully-automated-pipeline-generation-inside-salesforce)); PriceLabs uses a generator plus verifier LLM, language detection, case studies pulled per message, and sends every fourth sequence to Slack for human review before Smartlead ([PriceLabs](https://www.nrev.ai/customer-stories/how-pricelabs-built-an-ai-powered-outbound-engine)).

**Build modes:** describe the goal; the AI Builder picks trigger and action nodes, plans grouping, filters and enrichment, estimates credits per step, and asks the user to "Approve Plan and Create Workflow" ([AI Builder docs](https://docs.nrev.ai/building_workflows/ai_builder.md)). There is also a CLI for Claude Code ([home](https://www.nrev.ai)).

#### 4. Technical mechanisms and interesting approaches
- **Deterministic agents:** "AI is used where it adds value, not on every step"; fixed execution paths, "100% idempotent runs", checkpointing and resume from failure, visual trace, undo any step ([home](https://www.nrev.ai)). nRev contrasts this with open-ended agents like Relevance AI.
- **Node library (docs):** AI Toolkit (Ask AI, AI Ranker, AI Scorer, Summariser, AI Web Scraping, Classifier); people and company search and enrichment; waterfall email and phone enrichment; Fetch Jobs, Company News, Technologies, Customers, Partners, Vendors; eight LinkedIn scrapers (post reactors, comments by person, posts by company); Google Search and Google Jobs; scheduler and webhook triggers; custom code ([docs nav](https://docs.nrev.ai/)).
- **AI Scorer:** user writes scoring criteria in plain English, sets min and max, optional one-line REASON column; 1 credit per row, charged only on success; chainable (fit, then risk) ([AI Scorer](https://docs.nrev.ai/nodes/ai_toolkit/ai_scorer_node.md)).
- **"GTM Ontology" and "Knowledge House":** a domain data model plus a map of the customer's tools; "preferential API limits with GTM vendors" ([home FAQ](https://www.nrev.ai)). Details: Not published.
- **Competitor-rep social listening** as an intent source (novel).
- **Stack (job ad):** agents for outreach, campaign planning, market analysis, "intent analysis from search, web, and CRM data"; RAG, token and context-window optimisation; "open-source models" experience asked for; Python, TypeScript, SQL ([Antler posting](https://careers.antler.co/companies/nurturev-2/jobs/55625161-gtm-engineer)). The launch post mentions a CLI with "70+ data providers" ([hunted.space, third-party](https://hunted.space/product/nrev)) (search extract).
- Data vendors, match rates and models used: Not published. Case studies show Google Sheets as the operational store ([Mysa](https://www.nrev.ai/customer-stories/how-mysa-io-built-an-ai-powered-intent-engine-for-pipeline-growth)).

#### 5. Why they get the results they get
- **Wedge:** RevOps teams who would otherwise wire Clay, n8n and point tools themselves; nRev sells proven plays, not a blank canvas.
- **Service-assisted delivery:** bundled GTM hours and "we implement this for you" offers mean nRev staff build much of the result. Inference: results depend on that team.
- **Cost transparency:** visible per-node credits and free testing reduce the risk of trying.
- **Weaknesses:** small brand reach and spreadsheet-based case-study infrastructure. Inference: nRev scales by repeating builds rather than through a data advantage.

#### 6. What we can learn and adopt
- **Prospecting:** add hiring signals from public job boards (roles like maintenance manager or plant engineer signal investment for industrial buyers) and a "competitor engagement" signal.
- **Lead scoring:** adopt nRev's three-phase pattern: collect raw evidence, have AI extract structured events (expansion, key hire, funding, launch), then score 0 to 100 with weights and a written rationale. Our decaying-signal score can take the structured events as inputs.
- **Lead scoring:** let users write a plain-English scoring rule with an optional REASON column, as a second opinion beside our rules.
- **NBA and NBC:** present each NBC as a fixed, visible plan (steps, expected volume, estimated cost) that the user approves before it runs, like the AI Builder's "Approve Plan" step.
- **Content creation:** run a generator then a verifier pass on drafts, and route a sample (1 in 4) to a human reviewer.
- **Content mapping to each outreach sequence step:** attach "talking points tied to real events" to each step, so the message references the signal that triggered it.
- **Follow-up and response tracking:** pause the sequence automatically on reply and alert a human, with one message at a time per contact.

### 4 and 16. Leadsforge
**Website:** [getleadsforge.ai](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/) (leadsforge.ai redirects there) | **Founded / HQ:** product of Salesforge, founded by Frank Sondors; Estonian startup ([AIN, third-party](https://en.ain.ua/2024/04/24/salesforge-secures-500k-pre-seed-funding)); Leadsforge launched around March 2025 ([Salesforge LinkedIn](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/salesforgeai_1-search-engine-to-find-the-best-leads-activity-7307702305204666368-zE0-)) | **Category:** AI lead finder and waterfall enrichment; the data layer of the Salesforge "Forge" outbound stack

**Identification.** Entry 4's tagline "#1 Search Engine To Find The Best Leads" is the exact title of Salesforge's LinkedIn launch post introducing Leadsforge ([Salesforge LinkedIn](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/salesforgeai_1-search-engine-to-find-the-best-leads-activity-7307702305204666368-zE0-)). Salesforge's own pages still carry the line: the Agent Frank page banner reads "Leadsforge.ai - Search Engine To Find The Best Leads" ([Agent Frank](https://www.salesforge.ai/agent/frank)), and the help centre footer lists "Leadsforge - Search Engine For Leads" linking to leadsforge.ai ([help article](https://help.salesforge.ai/en/articles/10333627-signals-build-prospect-lists-based-on-real-world-buying-signals)). A third party also attributes the phrase to Leadsforge ([Landbase, third-party](https://www.landbase.com/blog/best-ai-agents-that-turn-plain-english-into-qualified-leads)). leadsforge.ai now resolves to getleadsforge.ai, titled "AI Lead Finder With 500M+ Contacts" ([site](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/)). Conclusion: entries 4 and 16 are one product. Confidence: high.

**Differentiator:** Leadsforge is not differentiated by owning data; it aggregates 20+ providers in a waterfall and charges only for verified results. What is distinctive is its position as the first step of a vertically integrated outbound stack (data, mailboxes, warm-up, sequencer, AI SDR), shipping weekly, with every capability exposed through API, MCP and CLI, plus evidence attached to each signal match and per-criterion qualification verdicts used to gate paid enrichment.

**Source coverage:** Website: read ([site](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/), [Agent Frank](https://www.salesforge.ai/agent/frank)) | Pricing: read in pass 1 ([pricing](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/pricing)); Agent Frank pricing read ([Agent Frank](https://www.salesforge.ai/agent/frank)) | Docs: read ([Signals help article](https://help.salesforge.ai/en/articles/10333627-signals-build-prospect-lists-based-on-real-world-buying-signals), [API swagger linked from changelog](https://api.leadsforge.ai/public/swagger/index.html#/maps-discovery) not read) | Changelog: read ([Salesforge changelog](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs), August to October 2026; earlier entries search extract only) | Blog: read in pass 1 ([API guide](https://www.salesforge.ai/blog/leadsforge-api)) | Case studies: read, Forge-level only ([Agent Frank page](https://www.salesforge.ai/agent/frank): VAI Consulting, Strongest Group) | Video: not found ("Watch demo" on [site](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/) not reachable) | Founder content: read in pass 1 ([launch post](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/salesforgeai_1-search-engine-to-find-the-best-leads-activity-7307702305204666368-zE0-)) | Funding and press: read in pass 1 ([Newsfile](https://www.newsfilecorp.com/release/206638)) | Jobs: search extract only ([Cutshort](https://cutshort.io/company/jobs-at-salesforge-🔥-02-HmPZBI4o), [startup.jobs](https://startup.jobs/company/salesforge)) | Integrations: read ([site](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/), [integration page](https://www.salesforge.ai/integrations/leadsforge) search extract) | Investor filings: not applicable

#### 1. Why they are performing well
**Verified facts**
- Parent Salesforge raised about $500k pre-seed in April 2024 ([Newsfile](https://www.newsfilecorp.com/release/206638)) and is described as profitable on that funding ([Startup Hungary, third-party](https://startuphungary.io/past-events/first-monday-with-salesforge)).
- **Fast, public shipping cadence.** Weekly release notes show, for Leadsforge: CSV enrichment and better ICP search (March), language filters (April), exclude lists (May), Signals as a sourcing path (June), US-only hiring signals (July), qualification-based filtering and Signals inside Agent Frank (31 August), enrichment history per run (25 August), local business search in the public API (21 September), 183 company niches and B2B/B2C/B2G business-model filters (29 September), lookalikes with match scores and follower counts in the Chrome extension (12 October), more follower-search titles and an EU location (20 October) ([changelog](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs); earlier months search extract).
- Ecosystem bundling launched in September: buying infrastructure on Infraforge, Primeforge or Mailforge unlocks a free month of Salesforge Growth, described as "our first step toward making the different Forge products work together" ([changelog, 16 September](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs)).
- Forge-level case studies: VAI Consulting (Agent Frank) and Strongest Group, an agency booking "1,000+ meetings across 15 clients with the Forge Stack" ([Agent Frank](https://www.salesforge.ai/agent/frank)). Leadsforge-specific case studies: Not published.

**Vendor claims**
- "500M+ contacts", "20+ data providers", "4.7 rating on G2", "some of the most accurate lead data available", SOC 2 compliant ([site](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/)).

#### 2. How they promise results
- Speed and accuracy: "verified emails, phones, and LinkedIn back in seconds". "Spend credits only when a verified match comes back. Unused credits never expire" ([site](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/)).
- Pricing (pass 1, still current): searching free; 1 credit per email, 10 per phone; Essential $49 a month for 2,000 credits; 100 free credits ([pricing](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/pricing)). Re-enrichment of data already held is skipped and not charged ([changelog, 25 August](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs)).
- Downstream, Agent Frank is priced per "Active Contact": $499 a month for 1,000 active contacts (the FAQ on the same page says $599, an inconsistency), which the vendor equates to 2,000 to 2,500 new leads and 6,000 to 7,500 emails a month ([Agent Frank](https://www.salesforge.ai/agent/frank)).
- **Explicit fit statement:** Agent Frank suits ACVs of "$5K to $100K" and "3,000 businesses or more"; it is a "not-so-good fit" if "your average deal size is consistently in the six to seven-figure range", for long RFP cycles, Fortune 500 targets, or "a small number of highly bespoke, high-touch deals" ([Agent Frank](https://www.salesforge.ai/agent/frank)). This matters for our high-ticket client.

#### 3. How they generate their outputs
1. **Choose a sourcing path:** ICP chat search ("the way you would explain it to a colleague"), company lookalikes from closed-won domains, follower extraction from a company's LinkedIn page, signals, local business search by radius, or CSV upload ([site](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/)).
2. **Signals path:** pick a signal type (job change, acquisition, funding, investor; hiring in the US), set filters (signal period, location, seniority, funding round and amount, headcount, founded year), review the estimated count, then extract with credits ([Signals article](https://help.salesforge.ai/en/articles/10333627-signals-build-prospect-lists-based-on-real-world-buying-signals)).
3. **Company-to-lead matching:** for company signals, matched companies are joined to the people database and relevant decision-makers are surfaced ([Signals article](https://help.salesforge.ai/en/articles/10333627-signals-build-prospect-lists-based-on-real-world-buying-signals)).
4. **Evidence:** every extracted company and contact carries "supporting evidence explaining why it matched", viewable in a Details panel and reusable for personalisation ([Signals article](https://help.salesforge.ai/en/articles/10333627-signals-build-prospect-lists-based-on-real-world-buying-signals)).
5. **Qualify:** user-written qualification prompts return Positive, Negative or Not sure per criterion; lists can then be filtered (OR within a criterion, AND across criteria) so only qualifying leads are enriched or exported ([changelog, 31 August](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs)).
6. **Enrich:** waterfall across 20+ providers until a verified match ([site](https://www.getleadsforge.ai/)); each run logged with who, when, channels and credits ([changelog, 25 August](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs)).
7. **Hand-off in the Forge stack:** export to Salesforge (multichannel sequences with unlimited mailboxes and LinkedIn senders), HubSpot, Salesforce or CSV. Mailforge, Infraforge and Primeforge supply mailboxes; Warmforge warms them for about two weeks. Agent Frank can take a Leadsforge signal, lookalike or upload as its source, research each prospect "their company, buying signals and timing, plus likely pain points and objections", write in one of nine tones and 20+ languages from a customer-supplied knowledge base, and run in Auto-Pilot or Co-Pilot (review before send) ([Agent Frank](https://www.salesforge.ai/agent/frank); [changelog, 31 August](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs)).

#### 4. Technical mechanisms and interesting approaches
- **Waterfall enrichment and charge-on-success**, with credits reserved upfront and refunded if unused; the same pattern was rolled out to email validation in September ([changelog, 29 September](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs); [API guide](https://www.salesforge.ai/blog/leadsforge-api)). Provider list and ordering: Not published.
- **Qualify before enrich** as a cost gate: per-criterion LLM verdicts filter which rows reach paid enrichment ([changelog](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs)).
- **Deliverability as part of the output.** Megaforge spreads sending across Gmail, Outlook, Mailforge and Infraforge mailboxes (about 15 emails a day each) "so if one burns, there's a fallback"; ESP matching, bounce shield and a spam-check API sit in Salesforge ([Agent Frank](https://www.salesforge.ai/agent/frank); [changelog, 5 and 20 October](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs)). LinkedIn limits now pause only the throttled action type for six hours ([changelog, 29 September](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs)).
- **Agent memory from feedback:** "Your feedback helps shape the memories Frank uses in future outreach" ([Agent Frank](https://www.salesforge.ai/agent/frank)). Mechanism: Not published.
- **Agent-ready access:** REST, MCP and CLI for search, enrichment, followers, lookalikes and maps discovery, with async jobs and webhooks ([changelog, 21 September](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs); [API guide](https://www.salesforge.ai/blog/leadsforge-api)).
- **Taxonomy:** 183 niches across 25 domains, cross-industry niches such as "AI and Machine Learning" in manufacturing ([changelog, 29 September](https://roadmap.salesforge.ai/en/changelogs)).
- **Stack:** a Salesforge engineering posting is tagged Go, AWS and PostgreSQL and asks "how do you orchestrate millions of AI-driven interactions reliably?" ([Cutshort](https://cutshort.io/company/jobs-at-salesforge-🔥-02-HmPZBI4o)) (search extract). Models: Not published.

#### 5. Why they get the results they get
- **Distribution through the Forge family:** cross-sold to Salesforge's outbound users; one-click export into sequences; bundles across products.
- **Low-friction pricing:** free search, pay per verified hit, credits never expire.
- **Shipping velocity:** weekly releases close gaps against Apollo and Clay quickly.
- **Commodity data:** Inference: the edge is aggregation, packaging and deliverability, not proprietary data. Their own fit statement excludes six and seven-figure, high-touch deals.

#### 6. What we can learn and adopt
- **Qualified lead identification:** store "evidence for why this matched" on every account and signal, and show it next to the recommendation.
- **Lead scoring:** add per-criterion verdicts (Positive, Negative, Not sure) from user-written questions, and let users filter by them with AND across criteria. "Not sure" is a useful third state for thin industrial data.
- **Prospecting:** add closed-won lookalikes and "followers of a competitor's LinkedIn page" as list sources; add acquisition signals (new owners often review suppliers).
- **Segmentation:** use a finer niche taxonomy below industry, since industrial clients sell into narrow niches.
- **Campaign execution:** recommend a send volume per mailbox and per LinkedIn account in NBC plans, and warn when a plan needs more senders, as Agent Frank's allocation check does.
- **Deal:** take Salesforge's own fit statement as positioning evidence: volume AI SDRs say they do not fit six-figure, bespoke deals, which is the space Demand AI's pilot occupies.

#### 7. What I liked about their approach
_To be completed._

### 5. kōdlo
**Website:** [kodlo.ai](https://kodlo.ai) | **Founded / HQ:** built by a small team at the software firm Reinode ([Indie Hackers](https://www.indiehackers.com/product/k-dlo)); site live from May 2026, Product Hunt launch 11 October 2026 ([MakerStack, third-party](https://makerstack.co/reviews/kodlo-review/)) (search extract); co-founder Andriana Oprysko ([kōdlo blog byline](https://kodlo.ai/blog/single-agent-vs-multi-agent-ai-sales-tools/)); header says "New York" but core data is hosted in the EU (Hetzner, Germany and Finland) ([pricing FAQ](https://kodlo.ai/pricing/)) | **Category:** agent-native CRM and AI sales workspace for high-ticket and technical B2B sales

**Differentiator:** kōdlo is built for low-volume, high-value selling: one agent with one shared memory that interviews the user to write the ICP, finds prospects only from public sources with a cited reason for each, writes cited research briefs, answers technical questions from uploaded datasheets and price lists, and builds offers and decks on the customer's own template. It never sends and never writes to the CRM without a human click, and its source lists are assembled by the system from retrieved passages rather than written by the model.

**Source coverage:** Website: read ([home](https://kodlo.ai/), [features](https://kodlo.ai/features/), [AI prospecting](https://kodlo.ai/ai-prospecting/), [AI sales agent](https://kodlo.ai/ai-sales-agent/)) | Pricing: read ([pricing](https://kodlo.ai/pricing/)) | Docs: not found | Changelog: not found | Blog: read, 1 post ([single vs multi-agent](https://kodlo.ai/blog/single-agent-vs-multi-agent-ai-sales-tools/)) | Case studies: not found | Video: not found | Founder content: read in pass 1 ([Indie Hackers](https://www.indiehackers.com/product/k-dlo)); search extract only ([Product Hunt maker comment](https://www.producthunt.com/posts/kodlo-2)) | Funding and press: not found (no funding announcement); launch coverage search extract only ([AItoolly, third-party](https://aitoolly.com/ai-news/article/2026-10-12-kdlo-launches-unified-ai-sales-workspace-integrating-persistent-context-and-model-context-protocol-s)) | Jobs: not found | Integrations: read ([features](https://kodlo.ai/features/): MCP clients, Telegram and Google Chat on [home](https://kodlo.ai/)) | Investor filings: not applicable

#### 1. Why they are performing well
**Verified facts**
- Very early. The founder's launch post (29 August 2026) says "We have almost no users yet" and describes "a few engineers and one marketer" ([Indie Hackers](https://www.indiehackers.com/product/k-dlo)).
- Product Hunt top daily post badge ([home](https://kodlo.ai/)); the product page shows 37 followers ([Product Hunt](https://producthunt.com/products/kodlo)) (search extract). Trustpilot 3.7 from a single review ([Trustpilot, third-party](https://uk.trustpilot.com/review/kodlo.ai)).
- The team uses the product itself: "our own sales team runs on Recurring Scheduled Jobs daily" ([blog](https://kodlo.ai/blog/single-agent-vs-multi-agent-ai-sales-tools/)).
- Funding, customers, case studies: Not published.

**Vendor claims**
- "Built for your high-ticket deals." "Research that used to take an hour per company takes a few minutes of reading a brief" ([home](https://kodlo.ai/); [AI prospecting](https://kodlo.ai/ai-prospecting/)).
- "As of mid-2026, we haven't seen a comparable tool in this category ship" scheduled, auditable agent work with one memory ([blog](https://kodlo.ai/blog/single-agent-vs-multi-agent-ai-sales-tools/)).

#### 2. How they promise results
- The promise is depth, trust and fewer lost threads, not volume: "I don't know who to write to", "I don't know what to say", "nothing accumulates", "I'm afraid of breaking something" are the problems it targets ([AI prospecting](https://kodlo.ai/ai-prospecting/)). A second page targets technical sales: salespeople "can't answer a technical question" and offers "need both technical and sales input" ([AI sales agent](https://kodlo.ai/ai-sales-agent/)). No quantified outcomes.
- **Pricing:** Free ($0, 1 seat, one-time AI credits, no time limit); Starter $49 a month (1 seat, adds MCP and note taker, OCR up to 1,000 pages); Pro $199 a month (up to 3 seats, 4x limits, long-term memory, "Agent Learning", 10,000 OCR pages); Custom ("custom AI agents built around your workflow", SSO, SLA, DPA). Quarterly billing 15% off. "No per-contact charges, no data add-ons" ([pricing](https://kodlo.ai/pricing/)). Note: the homepage headline "Pay for signal, not seats" sits beside per-seat plans, and the comparison table marks MCP as included on Free while the text says it starts on Starter. Inference: messaging is still settling.

#### 3. How they generate their outputs
kōdlo publishes its cycle as: ICP interview, lead sourcing, list enrichment, deep research, cited brief, personalised outreach, pipeline update, campaign content, closed deal ([home](https://kodlo.ai/)). In detail:
1. **ICP interview.** The agent "asks what you sell, who already buys it, what a good fit looks like and what a bad one looks like", then writes the profile "following the standard framework": firmographics, buying roles, in-market signals and disqualifiers. The user corrects it as it goes ([pricing FAQ](https://kodlo.ai/pricing/)). The result is stored as a campaign brief "the agent reads first", together with an outreach playbook ([features](https://kodlo.ai/features/)).
2. **Discovery.** The Discovery agent searches "the open web" for matching companies and people, up to 25 leads per run, and leaves out companies already in the campaign ([features](https://kodlo.ai/features/); [AI prospecting](https://kodlo.ai/ai-prospecting/)). Listed sources include press releases, patent filings, job postings, trademark registries, conference programmes, funding rounds, open repositories, public LinkedIn pages, podcast transcripts, tender notices, Companies House and SEC EDGAR, "re-indexed hourly" ([home](https://kodlo.ai/)). A third-party LinkedIn page says kōdlo "runs on Exa" for search ([LinkedIn, third-party](https://pl.linkedin.com/in/ivantelnov)) (search extract).
3. **Evidence per lead.** Each suggestion shows "Why it fits" (for example "B2B company; ships a public API; matches your profile") and "Evidence" with dated sources (for example "Company blog: Public API launch, March; Careers page: Partnerships role, 6 days ago"). Results come back as a file with the person, role, LinkedIn, company, reasoning and source links; "if the results are off, tell the agent what's wrong and it narrows the search" ([AI prospecting](https://kodlo.ai/ai-prospecting/); [pricing FAQ](https://kodlo.ai/pricing/)).
4. **Human approval to import.** "Nothing is imported until you approve it." The reason each prospect was found stays on its timeline ([features](https://kodlo.ai/features/); [AI prospecting](https://kodlo.ai/ai-prospecting/)).
5. **Deep research.** The agent plans the question, runs searches, cross-checks and cites "on screen, step by step". "Every line traces to a source", and if a run hits its budget "the report names the gaps". Reports are saved as documents with stable links and can go to the Knowledge Hub ([home](https://kodlo.ai/); [features](https://kodlo.ai/features/)).
6. **Knowledge Hub.** Upload price lists, case studies, contracts, datasheets and manuals (PDF, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, CSV, text; scans via OCR). Answers carry "numbered footnotes and a Sources list naming each file and section". Projects can be private even from admins ([AI sales agent](https://kodlo.ai/ai-sales-agent/)).
7. **Draft with an angle.** The first message combines public research and Knowledge Hub proof; the UI shows "Why this angle?" as three parts: recent trigger, relevant signal, your proof ([AI prospecting](https://kodlo.ai/ai-prospecting/)). Drafts are "checked for machine-sounding phrasing" and use per-person tone presets (for example "founder to founder" or "a formal reply to procurement") ([features](https://kodlo.ai/features/)). The user sends from their own account.
8. **Offers and decks.** "An engineer gives the technical details. The agent writes the offer document from your documentation, or builds a deck on a template made from your own PowerPoint file or website" ([AI sales agent](https://kodlo.ai/ai-sales-agent/)).
9. **Pipeline and follow-up.** Contacts carry a status and dated timeline; only a person can mark "do not contact". Every proposed CRM change (create contact, log event, update campaign, open task) appears as a card the user must confirm; "the agent has no way to approve its own proposal" ([features](https://kodlo.ai/features/); [AI sales agent](https://kodlo.ai/ai-sales-agent/)). Call notes go to the Knowledge Hub and feed the follow-up ([features](https://kodlo.ai/features/)).
10. **Scheduled jobs.** "Every Monday, check these accounts for news and open a ticket if anything moved", with results pushed to Telegram or Google Chat ([features](https://kodlo.ai/features/); [blog](https://kodlo.ai/blog/single-agent-vs-multi-agent-ai-sales-tools/)). Wins and losses "feed back to refine the ICP" ([home diagram caption](https://kodlo.ai/)).

#### 4. Technical mechanisms and interesting approaches
- **One agent, semantic tool selection per turn.** Rather than named bots, the agent "retrieves only the tools relevant to this question, this turn, by semantic search across the library", so the toolbox can grow without bloating the prompt ([blog](https://kodlo.ai/blog/single-agent-vs-multi-agent-ai-sales-tools/)).
- **Plays as Markdown files.** A "skill library" of plain-Markdown descriptions of recurring jobs (first-touch message, prospect dossier, LinkedIn comment); "editing a play is editing a document" ([blog](https://kodlo.ai/blog/single-agent-vs-multi-agent-ai-sales-tools/)).
- **System-assembled citations.** "The list of sources under the answer is assembled by the system from the passages it actually retrieved. It is not written by the model, so it cannot cite a document it did not read" ([AI sales agent](https://kodlo.ai/ai-sales-agent/)). This is a concrete anti-hallucination design.
- **Three-layer memory** (short-term, long-term session, user profiling) shared by all capabilities; checkpointed, resumable chats; users can view, pause and delete memory; zero-training policy ([home](https://kodlo.ai/); [blog](https://kodlo.ai/blog/single-agent-vs-multi-agent-ai-sales-tools/); [pricing FAQ](https://kodlo.ai/pricing/)).
- **Approval cards as architecture**, not a setting: write actions are proposals only ([AI sales agent](https://kodlo.ai/ai-sales-agent/)).
- **Public-source-only data** with a documented "legitimate interest" basis ([home](https://kodlo.ai/)).
- **MCP server** exposing prospects, documents and teammates' comments to Claude, Codex and others ([features](https://kodlo.ai/features/)).
- Models, fit-ranking method and how hourly re-indexing works: Not published.

#### 5. Why they get the results they get
- Inference: the wedge is research depth and trust for deals where one well-researched message matters more than scale; citations and approval gates reduce the fear of "sending something silly to the one company that could have been a customer" ([AI prospecting](https://kodlo.ai/ai-prospecting/)).
- The technical-sales angle (answering from datasheets, engineer-plus-agent offers) matches complex industrial products, but there is no published proof yet.
- Low price and a free tier suit founders; there is no evidence of traction beyond launch.

#### 6. What we can learn and adopt
- **Prospecting:** add tenders, patents, trademark filings, conference exhibitor lists and company registries (Companies House) as signal sources for industrial ICPs.
- **Segmentation:** start each campaign with a short agent-led ICP interview that ends in a written brief: firmographics, buying roles, in-market signals, disqualifiers. Our knock-outs and micro-segments should be generated from, and traceable to, that brief.
- **Qualified lead identification:** show "Why it fits" plus dated evidence links per account, and require a human to import or discard each suggestion.
- **NBA and NBC:** frame every recommended action with kōdlo's three-part "Why this angle?": recent trigger, relevant signal, our proof (case study or datasheet).
- **Content creation:** let the content studio answer and draft from uploaded datasheets, price lists and case studies with file-and-section citations assembled by the system, not the model; generate offers and decks on the client's own template.
- **Content mapping to each outreach sequence step:** attach one proof asset from the content library to each step, chosen to match the trigger.
- **Follow-up and response tracking:** add scheduled account-watch jobs that open a task when something changes, and feed win and loss reasons back to refine the ICP brief.

#### 7. What I liked about their approach
_To be completed._
