### 28. Nooks
**Website:** [nooks.ai](https://www.nooks.ai/) | **Founded / HQ:** late 2020, San Francisco (offices also in Seattle and Ireland); founders Dan Lee (CEO), Rohan Suri (CPO), Nikhil Cheerla (CTO) ([Series B post](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/series-b), [careers](https://www.nooks.ai/careers)) | **Category:** AI sales assistant platform, now positioned as "revenue infrastructure for reps and agents" (AI parallel dialer, AI sequencing, signals, coaching, revenue agents)

**Differentiator:** Nooks won adoption with a parallel dialer that reps "live in" for hours a day, then used that daily usage and call data as the base for signals, sequencing and agents. It is the most transparent company in this batch about how its agents are evaluated: it published its own sales benchmark, calibrated confidence flags, a backtested "strategy evaluator" and a per-rep style memory that turns edits into reusable skills. It positions explicitly against replacing reps, keeping humans on live conversations.

**Source coverage:**
Website/product pages: read ([homepage](https://www.nooks.ai/)) | Pricing: read (quote only; package contents) ([pricing](https://www.nooks.ai/pricing)) | Docs/help centre: search extract only ([Salesforce guide](https://nooks.help.usepylon.com/articles/4503347328-salesforce-sfdc-guide)) | Changelog: read (product updates index, 26 posts May 2024 to May 2026) ([product updates](https://www.nooks.ai/categories/product-releases)) | Blog/engineering blog: read, 4 posts in full ([agents research](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/building-agents-for-sales), [revenue agents](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/the-next-era-of-revenue-agents), [AI Sequencing launch](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/introducing-ai-sequencing-the-evolution-of-intelligent-outbound), [Series B](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/series-b)) | Case studies: read, 1 in full ([Pendo](https://www.nooks.ai/customer-success/high-velocity-outbound-how-pendo-scaled-pipeline-productivity-with-nooks)) plus summary cards for 9 others | Video/YouTube: search extract only (title and description of [Pendo video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4QWi-Wt09QA)) | Founder posts/podcasts: read (CEO and CTO blog posts); search extract only ([podcast page](https://www.nooks.ai/news-media/why-ai-parallel-dialing-is-the-future-of-outbound), [Unite.AI](https://www.unite.ai/dan-lee-ceo-co-founder-of-nooks-interview-series/)) | Funding/press: read ([Series B](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/series-b)), search extract ([FullyRamped acquisition](https://pulse2.com/nooks-acquires-fullyramped-to-expand-revenue-agent-platform/)) | Jobs: read, 50+ listings and 1 posting in full ([careers](https://www.nooks.ai/careers), [Agent Engineer](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/nooks/9cd1d086-d20c-471a-af7d-6b08a3b90558)) | Integrations/marketplaces: read (pricing FAQ), search extract ([HubSpot marketplace](https://ecosystem.hubspot.com/marketplace/listing/nooks)) | Investor filings: not applicable

#### 1. Why they are performing well
**Verified facts**
- Funding: $5M seed, $22M Series A, then $43M Series B led by Kleiner Perkins with Lachy Groom and Tola Capital (24 October 2024), $70M in total ([Series B post](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/series-b)). No later round found; a 2026 job posting still says "over 70M USD" ([Agent Engineer](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/nooks/9cd1d086-d20c-471a-af7d-6b08a3b90558)).
- Acquired FullyRamped, an AI roleplay and coaching voice-agent start-up, in late August or early September 2026; its founder Aaron Marks joined Nooks' product team; terms not disclosed (third-party) ([Pulse 2.0](https://pulse2.com/nooks-acquires-fullyramped-to-expand-revenue-agent-platform/)).
- Team: "200+ person team"; around 40 engineers ([careers](https://www.nooks.ai/careers), [Agent Engineer](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/nooks/9cd1d086-d20c-471a-af7d-6b08a3b90558)). About 54 open roles across engineering, customer success, GTM (including EMEA from Ireland), finance and people ([careers](https://www.nooks.ai/careers)).
- Named customers with on-record quotes: HubSpot, Deel, Greenhouse, Spring Health, Pendo, Drata, Coder, Relay, Observe, Replit, plus logos for Notion, Cursor, Rippling, Vercel and Intercom ([homepage](https://www.nooks.ai/), [careers](https://www.nooks.ai/careers)).
- HubSpot marketplace listing with roughly 900 to 1,400 installs depending on snapshot, rating 3.6 from 11 reviews (search extract) ([HubSpot](https://ecosystem.hubspot.com/marketplace/listing/nooks)).

**Vendor claims**
- "1,800+ revenue teams" (site metadata); "over 2000 customers" and "at least tripled ARR each year for 3 years running" (job posting); "4X YoY" (careers page, which also still says "1,000+") ([homepage](https://www.nooks.ai/), [Agent Engineer](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/nooks/9cd1d086-d20c-471a-af7d-6b08a3b90558), [careers](https://www.nooks.ai/careers)).
- Users spend "4+ hours daily... more than Slack or their CRM" ([careers](https://www.nooks.ai/careers)).
- 4.8 rating from 1,700+ reviews (homepage badge; source platform not named) ([homepage](https://www.nooks.ai/)).

#### 2. How they promise results
- Outcome headlines per motion (vendor claims): "3x more meetings" (outbound), "20% faster speed to lead" (inbound), "10hrs saved per rep per week" (deal management), "50% faster ramp" (coaching), "2x more email replies with Nooks AI Sequencing" ([homepage](https://www.nooks.ai/)).
- Customer metrics (vendor claims): HubSpot 24% pipeline uplift per BDR and 67% more meetings; Greenhouse 70% more opportunities; Spring Health 4x meeting rate; Drata 25% more meetings from AI signals; Relay 90% SDR time savings; Deel 600+ conversations in 5 days ([careers](https://www.nooks.ai/careers)).
- Pendo (read in full): one in three meetings sourced from AI signals; 3x quarter-on-quarter rise in meetings from AI emails; 85% more daily calls; 90% more phone meetings; 200% more meetings from Q2 to Q3 ([Pendo](https://www.nooks.ai/customer-success/high-velocity-outbound-how-pendo-scaled-pipeline-productivity-with-nooks)).
- The agent team promises to "5x their meetings booked within a 1 month trial" for Agent Studio design partners ([Agent Engineer](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/nooks/9cd1d086-d20c-471a-af7d-6b08a3b90558)), and Nooks says it "doubled meetings booked per sales rep for our own team" in three months ([agents research](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/building-agents-for-sales)).
- Pricing: custom quote only ([pricing](https://www.nooks.ai/pricing)). Third-party estimates of about $4,000 to $5,000 per user per year list price with five-seat minimums (third-party, unverified) ([JustCall](https://justcall.io/hub/cost/nooks-pricing/), [ColdIQ](https://coldiq.com/blog/nooks-pricing)). Priced per seat, not per outcome; the free trial is the proof mechanism ([Series B post](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/series-b)).

#### 3. How they generate their outputs
1. **Data in:** CRM (bi-directional Salesforce and HubSpot sync), call recordings and transcripts, notes, emails, LinkedIn, third-party data and web research (job openings, landing pages); contact data through ZoomInfo, Cognism and LeadIQ pass-through, or a bundled data package (Wiza, People Data Labs, Apollo, Forager, Datagma, Prospeo) with waterfall enrichment ([pricing](https://www.nooks.ai/pricing), [Series B post](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/series-b)).
2. **Signals:** "100+ pre-built signals", signals from CRM and calls, a custom signal builder and intent scoring ([pricing](https://www.nooks.ai/pricing)). Pendo's GTM engineer built custom signals by "replicating what our best reps were already researching manually" and tunes them on rep feedback. A "Requested Follow-Up" signal is created when live transcription hears a prospect ask for a later touch, and it resurfaces at the right time ([Pendo](https://www.nooks.ai/customer-success/high-velocity-outbound-how-pendo-scaled-pipeline-productivity-with-nooks)).
3. **Prioritisation:** dynamic smartlists that auto-add or remove prospects, rule-based prioritisation, and an AI account assistant for account planning ([pricing](https://www.nooks.ai/pricing)).
4. **Actions:** parallel dialing with AI answer detection, voicemail drop, number rotation and live battlecards; multichannel sequences (call, email, SMS, LinkedIn); "Outbound Plays" that trigger automatically from buying signals ([pricing](https://www.nooks.ai/pricing), [AI Sequencing launch](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/introducing-ai-sequencing-the-evolution-of-intelligent-outbound)).
5. **Content:** AI emails drafted from the unified account context, including first-party conversation history, with a "rapid approval flow"; "the rep just handles the final context" ([pricing](https://www.nooks.ai/pricing), [Pendo](https://www.nooks.ai/customer-success/high-velocity-outbound-how-pendo-scaled-pipeline-productivity-with-nooks)).
6. **Cross-assistant loops:** the dialer detects follow-ups and referrals, which feed the prospecting assistant; roleplay is trained on real calls, and a rep can "try again" against a simulated version of a prospect they just called ([Series B post](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/series-b)).
7. **Agents (2026):** an AI Assistant across the product, with plan mode; Agent Studio aims to automate "90% of the non-calling, non-creative work" of an SDR team ([agents research](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/building-agents-for-sales), [Agent Engineer](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/nooks/9cd1d086-d20c-471a-af7d-6b08a3b90558)). Low-stakes steps such as updating opportunities run automatically; higher-stakes actions require rep approval ([revenue agents](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/the-next-era-of-revenue-agents)).

#### 4. Technical mechanisms and interesting approaches
All from the CTO's September 2026 research post unless stated ([agents research](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/building-agents-for-sales)):
- **SalesBench and SalesBench Verified:** an internal eval of factual sales questions. The agent scored 97% on the standard set, yet reps felt it was wrong "half the time". A harder subset built from customer traces with temporal drift, source disagreement and model disagreement scored below 50%, rising to 72 to 79% after rework (charted across Fable 5.0, GPT 5.6 Sol and Fable 5.1). Labellers judged conflicting reasoning traces rather than re-answering from scratch.
- **Calibrated confidence:** logprobs, ensemble disagreement and separate verifier models all lost to a carefully worded prompt asking for a verbalised confidence score; answers below threshold are flagged so reps double-check only those.
- **Filesystem-as-context:** CRM records, transcripts, notes and prospect data are materialised as files the agent searches with tools, following the coding-agent pattern, instead of one large context window.
- **Strategy evaluator:** editable "plan mode" failed because reps made inconsistent edits. Instead the agent searches similar accounts, proposes several strategies, and a backtested evaluator estimates each strategy's chance of closing from like-for-like historical accounts and prunes the weak ones.
- **Style guardrails and skills:** a negative set of 500+ bad AI messages with a rubric, applied as real-time correction guardrails ("decision-time guidance") rather than in the prompt; after heavy edits, the agent proposes a written skill describing how to generalise the edit, which the rep approves. Claimed result: "96% of emails sent autonomously without edits".
- **Platform framing:** Execution (dialer, deliverability, orchestration, guardrails), Context (account state, signals, memory, warm paths) and Learning loop (grading, attribution, evals, simulation) ([homepage](https://www.nooks.ai/)).
- **What jobs reveal:** forward-deployed "Agent Engineers" own customer revenue outcomes, share Slack channels with customers, and ship prompt or tool changes within a week; separate teams for Voice AI, Core Infrastructure and Agent Studio; "AI Deployment Strategist" roles in customer success ([careers](https://www.nooks.ai/careers), [Agent Engineer](https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/nooks/9cd1d086-d20c-471a-af7d-6b08a3b90558)). Programming languages and model vendors in production: Not published.
- Claimed training data of "over 100 million sales conversations" (author bio, vendor claim) ([Series B post](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/series-b)).

#### 5. Why they get the results they get
- **Wedge with daily habit:** calling shows ROI within days and keeps reps in the product for hours, which generates the richest top-of-funnel data ([Series B post](https://www.nooks.ai/blog-posts/series-b)).
- **First-party conversation data** feeds signals, emails and coaching, which point tools without a dialer cannot see.
- **Evaluation discipline:** they measure what reps actually trust, not just benchmark accuracy.
- **Forward-deployed engineers** tie the product team to customer meeting numbers.
- **Consolidation:** the pricing FAQ says AI Sequencing "fully replaces Outreach and Salesloft", with turnkey migration ([pricing](https://www.nooks.ai/pricing)).

#### 6. What we can learn and adopt
- **Lead scoring:** add a "requested follow-up" signal type that the user (or a call note) can log with a date, and resurface the account automatically when it falls due.
- **Qualified lead identification:** interview the client's best rep about what they research manually, and turn the top three items into custom signals; Pendo got a third of meetings this way.
- **NBA and NBC:** for each recommended action, show a confidence flag and only ask the human reviewer to check flagged ones, rather than every recommendation.
- **NBA and NBC:** rank candidate actions by how similar accounts responded in the past (a simple lookalike evaluator over our own pilot history), and prune weak options before showing them.
- **Content creation:** keep a list of rejected or heavily edited drafts with reasons; when a reviewer edits heavily, propose a one-line style rule for approval and apply it next time.
- **Follow-up and response tracking:** treat phone as a first-class channel for high-ticket industrial buyers; log call outcomes as signals even if dialing happens outside Demand AI.

#### 7. What I liked about their approach

## Group B: Established and legacy players

### 20. Apollo.io
**Website:** [apollo.io](https://www.apollo.io/) | **Founded / HQ:** 2015, San Francisco (remote-first, hubs in Austin, London, Bangalore) ([Greenhouse](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio)) | **Category:** All-in-one sales intelligence and engagement platform, now "the AI GTM System"

**Differentiator:** Apollo's moat is a contributory data network refreshed by the bounces, replies and corrections of millions of users, sold at freemium prices so the network keeps growing. On top of that data it now ships an agent "harness", a signal-based execution layer and a plain-English app builder, and it distributes its data into other agents (Salesforce Hunter, Claude, ChatGPT) rather than only into its own interface.

**Source coverage:**
Website/product pages: read (pass 1) | Pricing: read ([pricing](https://www.apollo.io/pricing), pass 1) | Docs/help centre: read ([data overview](https://knowledge.apollo.io/hc/en-us/articles/19331318468621), pass 1) | Changelog: read ([release notes 2026](https://knowledge.apollo.io/hc/en-us/articles/43226752968077-Release-Notes-2026), pass 1; could not re-access in pass 2 due to rate limits) | Blog: read (customer stories) | Case studies: read, 2 first-party in full ([Noble](https://www.apollo.io/magazine/noble-customer-story), [Smartling](https://www.apollo.io/magazine/smartling-customer-story)), 1 search extract (Cyera, page now 404), index of 26 via aggregator ([casestudies.com](https://www.casestudies.com/vendor/apollo), third-party) | Video/YouTube: search extract only (channel linked from [aggregator](https://www.casestudies.com/vendor/apollo)) | Founder/leadership posts: read (CEO quotes in press releases) | Funding/press: read ([ApolloNEXT 2026 release](https://www.martech360.com/news/media-growth/apollo-reveals-the-ai-gtm-system-bringing-data-intelligence-and-execution-into-a-single-engine-eliminating-tool-sprawl-and-ushering-in-the-future-of-go-to-market), pass 1 sources) | Jobs: read, 54 listings and 2 postings in full ([board](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio), [Native Data](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6199327004), [Builder Studio](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6226635004)) | Integrations/marketplaces: read (pass 1: Salesforce Hunter, MCP connectors) | Investor filings: not applicable (private)

#### 1. Why they are performing well
**Verified facts**
- $100M Series D (August 2023) led by Bain Capital Ventures at $1.6B; about $250M raised in total ([Crunchbase News](https://news.crunchbase.com/sales-marketing/apollo-io-funding-sales-tech-unicorn/), third-party; restated in [job postings](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6199327004)).
- $150M ARR announced 29 May 2025 ([Apollo magazine](https://www.apollo.io/magazine/apollo-reaches-150-million-arr-fueled-by-ai)). Job postings describe Apollo as "profitable, growing fast, and rebuilding the product around AI and agents" ([Builder Studio](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6226635004)).
- ApolloNEXT 2026 (announced 1 October 2026): launch of Builder Studio, the Intelligence Layer (Intelligence Profile and GTM Harness) and Messaging OS; CEO Matt Curl keynote ([press release via MarTech360](https://www.martech360.com/news/media-growth/apollo-reveals-the-ai-gtm-system-bringing-data-intelligence-and-execution-into-a-single-engine-eliminating-tool-sprawl-and-ushering-in-the-future-of-go-to-market)). Availability by product: not confirmed first-party; one third-party analysis lists Builder Studio and Messaging OS as waitlist ([MarketBetter](https://marketbetter.ai/blog/gong-apollo-zoominfo-agent-builders/), third-party). The Builder Studio job posting confirms it is "in the hands of early design partners now and is heading toward broad launch" ([Builder Studio](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6226635004)).
- Launch data partner for Salesforce's Hunter outbound agent, GA planned November 2026 ([Apollo newsroom](https://www.apollo.io/newsroom/announcements/apollo-named-flagship-data-partner-for-hunter-salesforces-new-outbound-sales-agent)).
- Hiring (54 open roles): heavy SMB and mid-market sales hiring in Austin, Salt Lake City, London and Bangalore; partnerships roles including "Partner Manager, AI Ecosystems" and "Director, Data and Distribution Partnerships"; engineering in data and Builder Studio ([board](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio)).

**Vendor claims**
- 5 million users at over 600,000 companies, including "more than 100 teams at Fortune 500 companies" ([press release](https://www.martech360.com/news/media-growth/apollo-reveals-the-ai-gtm-system-bringing-data-intelligence-and-execution-into-a-single-engine-eliminating-tool-sprawl-and-ushering-in-the-future-of-go-to-market)). Database size is stated inconsistently: 240M contacts on the website versus "210 million B2B contacts and 35 million companies" in job postings ([Native Data](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6199327004)).
- AI Assistant: nearly 20,000 weekly active users and "2.3x more meetings" for beta users (March 2026) ([Apollo newsroom](https://www.apollo.io/newsroom/announcements/apollo-launches-ai-assistant-powering-end-to-end-agentic-workflows-in-the-first-ai-native-all-in-one-gtm-platform)).

#### 2. How they promise results
- Benchmarks: vendor-commissioned Tolly test reported 2.37% cold-to-meeting conversion versus a 0.5 to 1.5% range ([Apollo newsroom](https://www.apollo.io/newsroom/announcements/new-report-apollo-exceeds-industry-benchmarks-for-data-quality-and-cold-email-outreach)).
- Named case studies (first-party, vendor claims): Noble reached 80 to 90% enrichment coverage using the Enrichment API and Waterfall Enrichment, a 10% visitor-to-agreement conversion rate and 3x positive replies; its co-founder says it would otherwise have about 10% of the data it needs ([Noble](https://www.apollo.io/magazine/noble-customer-story)). Smartling's BDR leader ran "always on" research through engineered prompts in AI Power-ups to feed a prioritised pipeline, claiming 10x more personalised emails per BDR and new segments such as local government and life sciences ([Smartling](https://www.apollo.io/magazine/smartling-customer-story)). Cyera: 75% more meetings with 50% less effort, rolled out from a BDR pilot to 100+ reps (search extract; page now returns 404) ([Cyera](https://www.apollo.io/magazine/cyera-customer-story)).
- Aggregated story headlines (third-party index of first-party stories): Ashby 4x meetings; Aligned 40%+ cold call conversions; Census replaced ZoomInfo and Outreach; Emerge 400% pipeline growth; Lemonlight halved bounce rates; Superside 5x ARR; TraceLink and Autodesk multi-million-dollar deals ([casestudies.com](https://www.casestudies.com/vendor/apollo), third-party).
- Pricing is seat plus credits, not outcomes: Free; Basic $49; Professional $79; Organization $119 per seat per month billed annually (minimum three seats); credits and AI features scale by tier ([pricing](https://www.apollo.io/pricing)).

#### 3. How they generate their outputs
1. **Data acquisition:** contributor network (2M+ people sharing contacts), engagement feedback (bounces, replies), web crawlers, third-party providers ([data overview](https://knowledge.apollo.io/hc/en-us/articles/19331318468621)). Job postings add the browser extension, customer CRMs and "indexing people and company data from across the internet" as inputs ([Native Data](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6199327004)).
2. **Freshness management:** systems to detect when data is stale or out of sync and decide when to refresh; feedback loops where "bounced emails, stale phone numbers, and customer corrections continuously improve Apollo's underlying data"; quality benchmarks for coverage, confidence and freshness per data vector ([Native Data](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6199327004)).
3. **Intelligence Profile:** a continuously updated, unified and enriched view of every contact and account ([press release](https://www.martech360.com/news/media-growth/apollo-reveals-the-ai-gtm-system-bringing-data-intelligence-and-execution-into-a-single-engine-eliminating-tool-sprawl-and-ushering-in-the-future-of-go-to-market)).
4. **Signals and scoring:** Bombora intent topics (since June 2026), website visitors, job changes; "Enhanced Scoring" builder (February 2026) ([release notes](https://knowledge.apollo.io/hc/en-us/articles/43226752968077-Release-Notes-2026)). Scoring model: Not published.
5. **GTM Harness:** "specialized agents and skills spanning prospecting, research, sequencing, workflows, enrichment, analytics, deliverability", combined with company GTM context, orchestration, memory and governance, to produce "data-backed recommendations and actions" ([press release](https://www.martech360.com/news/media-growth/apollo-reveals-the-ai-gtm-system-bringing-data-intelligence-and-execution-into-a-single-engine-eliminating-tool-sprawl-and-ushering-in-the-future-of-go-to-market)).
6. **Messaging OS:** captures buying signals and decides "who to reach, when and through which channel", coordinating sales and marketing to avoid overlapping outreach, learning "with every send" ([same](https://www.martech360.com/news/media-growth/apollo-reveals-the-ai-gtm-system-bringing-data-intelligence-and-execution-into-a-single-engine-eliminating-tool-sprawl-and-ushering-in-the-future-of-go-to-market)).
7. **Scheduled agents and approval:** daily, weekly or monthly agents for discovery, sequence creation and optimisation, with users reviewing results; guardrails on high-impact actions such as sending ([release notes](https://knowledge.apollo.io/hc/en-us/articles/43226752968077-Release-Notes-2026)).
8. **Builder Studio:** customers describe a tool in plain English; an agent plans it and generates code made of Pages, Databases, Workflows and Agents, with Apollo data, sequences, deliverability and CRM sync as native blocks ([Builder Studio](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6226635004)).

#### 4. Technical mechanisms and interesting approaches
- **Data as an engineering organisation:** a Senior Director role owns Native Data, Waterfall Enrichment, and Scraping and Extension, covering "large-scale enrichment, scraping, ETL, reverse ETL, and data synchronization", vendor selection and diversification, and win/loss analysis by data quality ([Native Data](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6199327004)). The posting frames latency as value: "the more fresh the data, the more valuable it is in triggering outreach".
- **Builder Studio runtime:** generated apps run on Cloudflare Workers and Durable Objects with delegated identity, so each app acts "with exactly the access the user has"; explicit split between deterministic workflow steps and agentic steps; tests and evals owned by the team ([Builder Studio](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6226635004)).
- **Stack (from postings):** TypeScript and React; Ruby on Rails and Node.js; Python, MongoDB and Elasticsearch; Kubernetes, Docker, Terraform; GCP ([Builder Studio](https://job-boards.greenhouse.io/apolloio/jobs/6226635004)). ML roles mention Databricks, Snowflake, MLflow and Airflow and models for search, recommendations and content generation (search extract) ([Bulldogjob](https://bulldogjob.pl/companies/jobs/188086-senior-machine-learning-engineer-apollo-io)). A Principal AI Engineer role covered "multi-agent systems" (search extract) ([Working Nomads](https://www.workingnomads.com/jobs/principal-ai-engineer-apolloio)).
- **Headless distribution:** MCP connectors for Claude, ChatGPT, Perplexity, Cursor and Replit, and data supply to Salesforce Hunter (pass 1) ([release notes](https://knowledge.apollo.io/hc/en-us/articles/43226752968077-Release-Notes-2026)).
- Models used, scoring algorithm, Messaging OS channel-selection logic: Not published.

#### 5. Why they get the results they get
- **Network effect on data:** cheap seats grow the user base, which validates and refreshes the data, which attracts more users.
- **Execution plus data in one loop:** the agent can act on the data it holds, and every send teaches the data layer.
- **Distribution breadth:** freemium, partner programmes and other vendors' agents all route demand to Apollo.
- **Shipping speed:** monthly releases in 2026 and three major launches at ApolloNEXT.
- Caution (third-party): practitioner reviews report weaker accuracy outside North America ([Salesgear](https://www.salesgear.io/blog/apollo-io-review/), third-party), which matters for non-US industrial buyers.

#### 6. What we can learn and adopt
- **Prospecting:** record bounce, wrong-number and "left company" outcomes from Dripify and email back into our lead file, and mark each contact as verified, stale or dead with a date.
- **Qualified lead identification:** track coverage and freshness per field (email, phone, title) per segment, and show it on the upload screen.
- **Segmentation:** before recommending outreach to an account, check whether another campaign already touched it recently (Messaging OS's "no overlapping outreach" rule).
- **NBA and NBC:** run the NBA job on a weekly schedule and present a short list of proposed actions for approval, as Apollo's scheduled agents do.
- **Campaign execution:** keep deterministic steps (dedupe, knock-outs, scoring) separate from AI steps (copy, rationale) in our design, and label which is which in the UI.
- **Content creation:** copy Smartling's pattern: a saved, engineered research prompt per segment that runs on every new account and feeds the first-line personalisation.

#### 7. What I liked about their approach

### 21. ZoomInfo
**Website:** [zoominfo.com](https://www.zoominfo.com/) and [gtm.ai](https://gtm.ai/docs/mcp/tools) | **Founded / HQ:** 2007 (as DiscoverOrg) / Vancouver, Washington; Nasdaq: GTM ([10-K FY2025](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1794515/000179451526000012/zi-20251231.htm)) | **Category:** B2B data incumbent repositioning as an "all-in-one AI GTM platform" and a headless context layer for other vendors' agents

**Differentiator:** ZoomInfo is turning its contact and company graph into infrastructure that any agent can call (API, MCP server, open-source CLI) while also selling its own agent layer (GTM Studio plus Agent Teams). Two things stand out against the market: a published, versioned benchmark (GTM Bench) that grades agents on GTM work and penalises confident wrong answers, and a pricing unit ("Records Under Management") that charges once per record per year rather than per lookup.

**Source coverage:** Website: read ([GTM Studio](https://www.zoominfo.com/products/gtm-studio)) | Pricing: read, packaging only, list prices not published ([GTM Studio RUM section](https://www.zoominfo.com/products/gtm-studio)) | Docs: read ([GTM.AI MCP tools reference](https://gtm.ai/docs/mcp/tools)) | Changelog: not found as a page; MCP roadmap table read ([tools page](https://gtm.ai/docs/mcp/tools)) | Blog: search extract only ([Customer Impact Report 2025](https://pipeline.zoominfo.com/sales/zoominfo-customer-impact-report-2025-gtm-intelligence-era)) | Case studies: search extract only ([Copilot release 2024](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20240918574142/en)) | Video: titles only, Vidyard walkthroughs listed on [GTM Studio](https://www.zoominfo.com/products/gtm-studio) | Leadership: read, CEO and CPO quotes in releases; earnings calls via third-party summaries | Press: read ([Agent Teams](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20261001491566/en/ZoomInfo-Introduces-Agent-Teams-Powered-by-the-Acquisition-of-DoubleO.ai), [GTM.AI CLI and GTM Bench](https://www.destinationcrm.com/Articles/CRM-News/CRM-Across-the-Wire/ZoomInfo-Launches-the-GTM.AI-CLI-and-GTM-Bench-175690.aspx)) | Jobs: read, 1 posting in full plus 2 via search ([Principal ML Engineer](https://www.zoominfo.com/careers/8833507002)) | Integrations: read (integration list in releases; [marketplace](https://market.zoominfo.com/) link only) | Filings: read ([10-K FY2025](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1794515/000179451526000012/zi-20251231.htm), [Q2 2026 8-K](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001794515/000179451526000054/gtm-8kex991x20260805.htm)); Q2 10-Q search extract only ([10-Q](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001794515/000179451526000056/zi-20260630.htm))

#### 1. Why they are performing well
**Verified facts (filings and first-party releases)**
- FY2025 revenue $1,249.5M, up 3%; over 35,000 customers; 1,921 customers above $100k ACV; net revenue retention (NRR) 90%; 3,180 employees ([10-K FY2025](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1794515/000179451526000012/zi-20251231.htm)).
- Q2 2026 (reported 5 August 2026): revenue $310.4M, up 1.2%; GAAP operating loss of $622.0M driven by a $650.5M goodwill impairment; adjusted operating margin 35%; NRR 89%; $100k+ customers 1,891; 76% of ACV upmarket; FY2026 revenue guidance raised to $1.207bn to $1.217bn ([Q2 8-K](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001794515/000179451526000054/gtm-8kex991x20260805.htm)). Q3 2026 results are due 4 November 2026, so Q2 is the latest available ([ZoomInfo IR](https://ir.zoominfo.com/news-releases/news-release-details/zoominfo-report-third-quarter-2026-financial-results-nov-4-2026)).
- Restructuring: a global reduction of about 600 employees (around 20%), including closure of Israeli facilities, targeting roughly $60M of annual operating cost savings ([Q2 10-Q](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/0001794515/000179451526000056/zi-20260630.htm) (search extract); [Barchart](https://www.barchart.com/story/news/3648170/zoominfo-s-consumption-pivot-gets-its-first-real-scorecard), third-party). At Q1, guidance had been cut to $1.185bn to $1.205bn, partly attributed to consumption pricing changes ([Nasdaq call summary](https://www.nasdaq.com/articles/zoominfo-technologies-q1-earnings-call-highlights), third-party, search extract).
- Product velocity in 2026: GTM.AI headless layer GA (June), open-source GTM.AI CLI and GTM Bench (13 July), DoubleO.ai acquisition and Agent Teams (1 October), Workbounce knowledge layer (14 October), flagship data partner for Salesforce's Hunter agent (15 October), Exa integration (23 October) ([Business Wire newsroom list](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20261001491566/en/ZoomInfo-Introduces-Agent-Teams-Powered-by-the-Acquisition-of-DoubleO.ai)).

**Vendor claims**
- GTM Studio: "expansion plays that used to take 3 weeks now launch in 30 minutes"; teams responding in under 5 minutes convert "26% higher"; top teams run "3x more plays" ([GTM Studio](https://www.zoominfo.com/products/gtm-studio)).
- Copilot users reported about 25% more pipeline (2024 release) ([Business Wire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20240918574142/en), search extract). No 2026 Copilot-specific case study found.

**Reading:** a profitable, slow-growth incumbent that is cutting cost and moving to consumption while betting that its data becomes the default context for other people's agents. The 10-K itself lists AI-driven seat compression and LLM providers absorbing contact data as risks ([10-K](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1794515/000179451526000012/zi-20251231.htm)).

#### 2. How they promise results
- Outcomes are framed as speed (plays in minutes), response time and pipeline, not guaranteed results ([GTM Studio](https://www.zoominfo.com/products/gtm-studio)).
- Pricing is moving from seats to usage. Management targets a larger share of non-seat ACV (third-party summary, [Barchart](https://www.barchart.com/story/news/3648170/zoominfo-s-consumption-pivot-gets-its-first-real-scorecard)). In GTM.AI, search is free, enrichment consumes "Data Credits" and research tools consume "AI Credits" ([MCP tools](https://gtm.ai/docs/mcp/tools)). GTM Studio uses Records Under Management: pay once, re-pull or re-enrich free for a year; waterfall enrichment from 25+ vendors is included at no extra cost ([GTM Studio](https://www.zoominfo.com/products/gtm-studio)). Agent Teams needs "no new SKU or contract" ([Business Wire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20261001491566/en/ZoomInfo-Introduces-Agent-Teams-Powered-by-the-Acquisition-of-DoubleO.ai)).
- Proof by benchmark: in GTM Bench v1 (20+ jobs, four systems, three models), GTM.AI scored an index of 77, delivered 98% of the operator's work product, returned 478 verifiable records per 1,000 and cost $0.79 per task (vendor-run, rubrics published) ([DestinationCRM](https://www.destinationcrm.com/Articles/CRM-News/CRM-Across-the-Wire/ZoomInfo-Launches-the-GTM.AI-CLI-and-GTM-Bench-175690.aspx)).
- A commissioned Forrester TEI reports 316% ROI for enterprises (vendor-commissioned) ([Business Wire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20251204367765/en/Total-Economic-Impact-study-finds-ZoomInfo-Delivers-316-ROI-and-%247.6-Million-in-Benefits-Over-3-Years-for-Enterprises-with-Payback-in-Under-6-Months), search extract).

#### 3. How they generate their outputs
1. **Collect:** an ML data engine gathers, standardises, entity-matches and verifies data from millions of sources, plus a contributory network, a human research team and content-consumption intent ([10-K](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1794515/000179451526000012/zi-20251231.htm)).
2. **Resolve into the GTM Context Graph:** 100M+ companies, 500M contacts, billions of signals; every record carries a confidence score and lineage ([DestinationCRM](https://www.destinationcrm.com/Articles/CRM-News/CRM-Across-the-Wire/ZoomInfo-Launches-the-GTM.AI-CLI-and-GTM-Bench-175690.aspx)). Internal knowledge (call transcripts, emails, decks, account notes) is added through Workbounce ([Business Wire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20261001491566/en/ZoomInfo-Introduces-Agent-Teams-Powered-by-the-Acquisition-of-DoubleO.ai)).
3. **Build an audience:** GTM Studio "Audiences" is a canvas that unifies first and third-party data, takes natural-language prompts ("B2B SaaS companies in North America hiring CROs"), supports custom fields, AI scoring and "signal-based transformations"; AI enrichment columns can be defined per audience ([GTM Studio](https://www.zoominfo.com/products/gtm-studio); [MCP tools](https://gtm.ai/docs/mcp/tools)).
4. **Fill gaps:** waterfall enrichment evaluates alternative sources and returns only the highest-confidence value with source attribution ([GTM Studio FAQ](https://www.zoominfo.com/products/gtm-studio)).
5. **Trigger plays:** Workflows and Alerts detect signal or data changes and trigger routing, assignment or CRM updates; prebuilt plays include lookalike ICP, competitive tech uninstall, cross-sell, champion tracking, inbound acceleration ([GTM Studio](https://www.zoominfo.com/products/gtm-studio)).
6. **Decide the next action (Agent Teams):** specialised agents with their own role, instructions and tools share context and "decide the next best action from the current state of the record". Worked example: a former champion becomes CRO elsewhere; agents check customer status and ICP fit, reconstruct the champion's role in the old deal, map the new buying committee and draft outreach; a later pricing-page visit resumes the play with full context ([Business Wire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20261001491566/en/ZoomInfo-Introduces-Agent-Teams-Powered-by-the-Acquisition-of-DoubleO.ai)).
7. **Content:** after a closed-won deal, agents review transcripts, emails and CRM records to learn why the customer bought, draft a case study, find 10 non-customer competitors of that customer and send tailored outreach backed by the case study (same source).
8. **Deliver and learn:** recommended actions land in ZoomInfo Copilot; "every click, open, and reply" is said to tune messaging and timing ([GTM Studio](https://www.zoominfo.com/products/gtm-studio)). Humans set approvals and limits per play.

#### 4. Technical mechanisms and interesting approaches
- **Tool surface designed for agents:** 26 MCP tools split into free discovery (Search Companies, Search Intent, Search Scoops, Find Similar Companies, Find Recommended Contacts), paid enrichment (Enrich Company Signals bundles intent, news and scoops for up to 10 companies) and AI research (Account Research, Conversation Intelligence, Audience Analysis). A typical chain is Lookup, then Search, then Enrich. Website visitor identification is on the "future roadmap" ([MCP tools](https://gtm.ai/docs/mcp/tools)). Novel: free search plus paid enrichment makes agents cheap to explore with.
- **Evaluation published:** GTM Bench grades "Answer" (share of the work product delivered) and "Grounding" (share of returned data traced to a real, current source), with negative scores for confident wrong answers ([DestinationCRM](https://www.destinationcrm.com/Articles/CRM-News/CRM-Across-the-Wire/ZoomInfo-Launches-the-GTM.AI-CLI-and-GTM-Bench-175690.aspx)). Grounding is graded against ZoomInfo's own records, which ZoomInfo itself says is a reach measure, not an independent audit.
- **Agent orchestration:** "real-time evaluation architecture" from DoubleO.ai; agents pass "understanding, not outputs" between steps (CPO quote) ([Business Wire](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20261001491566/en/ZoomInfo-Introduces-Agent-Teams-Powered-by-the-Acquisition-of-DoubleO.ai)). Internal design: Not published.
- **What jobs reveal:** the Principal ML Engineer owns "the data graph, entity resolution, buying intent, and agent context", including agent memory, "semantic intent at web scale", evaluation with "validated LLM judges" and release gates, and decisions on when to self-host, distil or call a hosted model with "cost tracked per unit of work"; preferred skills include learned re-ranking (recall@k, MRR, nDCG), open-weight models in PyTorch and prompt-injection defence ([job posting](https://www.zoominfo.com/careers/8833507002)). Inference: ZoomInfo is building its own retrieval and ranking stack and is cost-sensitive about LLM calls.
- **Governance travels with data:** access control, lineage, AI policy and audit logging apply on every surface including the CLI ([DestinationCRM](https://www.destinationcrm.com/Articles/CRM-News/CRM-Across-the-Wire/ZoomInfo-Launches-the-GTM.AI-CLI-and-GTM-Bench-175690.aspx)).
- Models used and scoring algorithms: Not published.

#### 5. Why they get the results they get
- **Data depth and identity resolution** built over two decades, with confidence and lineage per record, which agents need to avoid hallucinated contacts.
- **Distribution by embedding:** live inside Agentforce (Hunter), HubSpot Breeze, Microsoft Copilot Studio, Claude, ChatGPT, Gong, LeanData and Google Workspace ([DestinationCRM](https://www.destinationcrm.com/Articles/CRM-News/CRM-Across-the-Wire/ZoomInfo-Launches-the-GTM.AI-CLI-and-GTM-Bench-175690.aspx)). This defends against seat compression by making ZoomInfo a consumption line item inside other tools.
- **Buy rather than build** for agent capability (DoubleO.ai, Workbounce).
- **Headwinds:** NRR below 90%, downmarket decline, a large impairment and a 20% headcount cut. Agent Teams is weeks old with one named user (Silverfort).

#### 6. What we can learn and adopt
- **Next Best Action (NBA) and Next Best Campaign (NBC):** recompute the recommended action from the account's current state each time a signal arrives (state machine plus rules), so a stalled or returning account resumes its play with history, rather than restarting a fixed sequence.
- **Deal:** build a "closed-won to lookalikes" play: from a won deal, extract why they bought, generate a short case study in the Content Studio, then list similar non-customer companies as new targets. This suits industrial buyers where peer references matter.
- **Lead scoring:** attach a confidence score and source to every enriched field and signal in the upload, and show it beside the score, mirroring per-record confidence and lineage.
- **Prospecting:** price or budget enrichment per record per year and cache it (re-use without re-paying), which keeps a small team's data costs predictable.
- **Qualified lead identification:** ship named, prebuilt plays for new-logo motions (lookalike ICP, champion job change, competitor uninstall) as templates users can switch on.
- **Follow-up and response tracking:** create a small internal benchmark of 20 to 30 real tasks (e.g. "find the plant manager at X") and grade our outputs on completeness and grounding, with a penalty for confident wrong answers, before each release.

#### 7. What I liked about their approach

### 22. Salesforce Einstein and Agentforce for Sales
**Website:** [salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent/) and [Hunter](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent/outbound/) | **Founded / HQ:** 1999 / San Francisco (NYSE: CRM) | **Category:** CRM incumbent with an agent platform (Agentforce, Data 360, Atlas, Agent Script, Einstein Trust Layer) and new "job-ready" agents

**Differentiator:** Salesforce runs its sales agents inside the system of record, so they inherit permissions, history and write-back, and it now packages them as named, long-running "job-ready" agents (Hunter for outbound) on a runtime with memory, durable execution and dynamic steering. It is the most explicit vendor about mixing deterministic rules with LLM reasoning (Agent Script) and about evaluation tooling (scorers, A/B tests, optimiser agents). Third-party data is brought in through partners (Data Vendor Gateway) rather than owned.

**Source coverage:** Website: read ([Hunter page](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent/outbound/), [product page](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent/)) | Pricing: read ([Sales pricing](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/pricing/)) | Docs: read in pass 1 ([help doc](https://help.salesforce.com/s/articleView?language=en_US&id=sales.sales_agent_sdr_intro.htm&type=5), [Trailhead](https://trailhead.salesforce.com/content/learn/modules/agentforce-sales-agents-quick-look/discover-agentforce-sales-agents), [determinism guide](https://www.salesforce.com/agentforce/levels-of-determinism/)) | Changelog: read ([21 Dreamforce 2026 announcements](https://www.salesforce.com/blog/dreamforce-2026-announcements/)) | Blog: read (same; [Trust Layer developer blog](https://developer.salesforce.com/blogs/2023/10/inside-the-einstein-trust-layer)) | Case studies: read, figures only ([job-ready agents release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/stories/agentforce-job-ready-ai-agents/)) | Video: titles only (Dreamforce sales keynote linked on [Hunter page](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent/outbound/)) | Leadership: read (Benioff and Washington quotes in [Q2 release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/press-releases/2026/08/26/fy27-q2-earnings/)); call via [third-party transcript](https://fortune.com/company/salesforce-com/earnings/q2-2027/) | Press: read | Jobs: search extract only ([Agentic Selling team listing](https://www.dice.com/job-detail/49ff77fa-1932-4fb5-9fe1-23a3dcde3211)) | Integrations: read (Data Vendor Gateway partner logos on [Hunter page](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent/outbound/)) | Filings: read ([Q2 FY27 release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/press-releases/2026/08/26/fy27-q2-earnings/), [investor deck](https://s205.q4cdn.com/626266368/files/doc_financials/2027/q2/CRM-Q2-FY27-Quarterly-Investor-Deck.pdf))

#### 1. Why they are performing well
**Verified facts (earnings materials)**
- Q2 FY27 (quarter to 31 July 2026): revenue $11.3bn, up 11% (including $456M from Informatica); cRPO $33.5bn, up 14%; FY27 guidance raised to $46.1bn to $46.4bn ([Q2 release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/press-releases/2026/08/26/fy27-q2-earnings/)).
- Agentforce ARR "exceeded $1.5 billion, up over 240%"; Agentforce plus Data 360 ARR nearly $3.9bn, up over 210% ([Q2 release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/press-releases/2026/08/26/fy27-q2-earnings/)). **Definition change:** "Effective Q2 FY27, Agentforce ARR includes our AI offerings, Slackbot and Headless 360" (same source). The Q1 figure was $1.2bn under the old definition ([Q1 release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/press-releases/2026/05/27/fy27-q1-earnings/)), so the quarter-on-quarter step is not like for like and the growth rate is flattered.
- 7.0bn Agentic Work Units to date, 3.2bn in Q2 (up 97% quarter on quarter); Data 360 ingested 104 trillion records in Q2, 82 trillion via Zero Copy; bookings of premium Agentforce editions more than doubled quarter on quarter ([Q2 release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/press-releases/2026/08/26/fy27-q2-earnings/)).
- Pending acquisitions of Contentful and Fin were expected to close in Q3 FY27 ([Q2 release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/press-releases/2026/08/26/fy27-q2-earnings/)). Qualified (the source of the Piper inbound agent) was reportedly acquired in April 2026 for about $1.2bn ([PPC Land](https://ppc.land/salesforces-hunter-agent-gets-demandbase-intent-data-with-no-extra-contract/), third-party).
- The legacy Sales Cloud revenue line grows 9% to 10% ([investor deck](https://s205.q4cdn.com/626266368/files/doc_financials/2027/q2/CRM-Q2-FY27-Quarterly-Investor-Deck.pdf)).

**Vendor claims**
- "60% of Perk's sales pipeline is built by its outbound sales agent, Hunter"; Asana's Piper agent drives 4x conversation volume ([job-ready agents release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/stories/agentforce-job-ready-ai-agents/)). Methodology: Not published.
- Salesforce's own sales org: agents contacted 130,000 untouched leads and created 3,200 opportunities in four months ([March 2026 announcement](https://www.salesforce.com/news/stories/agentforce-sales-announcement/)).

#### 2. How they promise results
- Pitch: capacity without headcount, and agents that "initiate work rather than waiting for a prompt" ([Hunter page](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent/outbound/)). Outcomes framed as pipeline created from neglected leads and rescued deals.
- **Pricing (updated since pass 1):** Sales editions are Starter $25, Pro $100, Core $195, Advanced $395 and Max $550 per user per month. Agentforce for Sales is an add-on "from $125 USD/user/month" for Core and Advanced, and included in Max. Flex Credits per org per year: 500K (Core), 1M (Advanced), 2.75M (Max) ([pricing page](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/pricing/)). Flex Credits price an agent action at 20 credits, about $0.10 ([May 2025 release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/press-releases/2025/05/15/agentforce-flexible-pricing-news/)). Hunter pricing: Not published; GA planned November 2026 ([Dreamforce recap](https://www.salesforce.com/blog/dreamforce-2026-announcements/)).
- Usage pricing, not outcome pricing.

#### 3. How they generate their outputs
**Hunter (outbound, pilot; GA November 2026):**
1. A rep or team sets a goal (e.g. "rescue my at-risk deals before quarter end"); Hunter turns it into a measurable goal and plan ([job-ready release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/stories/agentforce-job-ready-ai-agents/)).
2. It learns the company's playbook from CRM data, customer conversations, enablement content and seller activity patterns ([Hunter FAQ](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent/outbound/)).
3. When outside context is needed it pulls approved firmographic, intent and contact data via the Data Vendor Gateway (logos shown: ZoomInfo, Demandbase, Apollo, Dun & Bradstreet, Moody's, Exa, G2, SalesIntel, UserGems, Coresignal, Harmonic, HG Insights), "without managing separate vendor integrations or contracts" (same source). Demandbase data arrives through its MCP server at run time ([PPC Land](https://ppc.land/salesforces-hunter-agent-gets-demandbase-intent-data-with-no-extra-contract/), third-party).
4. It researches and prioritises prospects, drafts or sends outreach, progresses opportunities, prepares proposals and supports forecasts, selecting "the skill each step needs" ([Hunter FAQ](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent/outbound/)).
5. Customer-facing actions "can require rep approval"; every action is logged; it works within configured permissions and business rules (same source).
6. Memory ties goals and actions to account, contact and opportunity records, so later steps build on earlier ones.

**Earlier Agentforce Sales agents** (March 2026): prospecting agent (ranked, continuously refreshed prospect list, seller approves), engagement agent (formerly SDR agent; nurtures leads by email in suggestive or autonomous mode), account research, pipeline management, quoting, partner and Sales Coach (RAG over CRM for role-play) ([announcement](https://www.salesforce.com/news/stories/agentforce-sales-announcement/); [help doc](https://help.salesforce.com/s/articleView?language=en_US&id=sales.sales_agent_sdr_intro.htm&type=5); [Trailhead](https://trailhead.salesforce.com/content/learn/modules/agentforce-sales-agents-quick-look/discover-agentforce-sales-agents)). Inbound is now handled by Piper ([job-ready release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/stories/agentforce-job-ready-ai-agents/)).

#### 4. Technical mechanisms and interesting approaches
- **Long-horizon runtime:** memory across sessions, durable execution that resumes or course-corrects when a step fails, and dynamic steering from user feedback; Hunter is the first agent on it ([job-ready release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/stories/agentforce-job-ready-ai-agents/)).
- **Harness concept:** Hunter is a "sales-specific harness built into Agentforce Coworker": methodology, CRM connections, a skills library and approval rules ([Hunter FAQ](https://www.salesforce.com/sales/ai-sales-agent/outbound/)). AI Skills let a user teach a task once and reuse it across agents ([Dreamforce recap](https://www.salesforce.com/blog/dreamforce-2026-announcements/)).
- **Hybrid reasoning:** Atlas runs a reason, act, observe loop; Agent Script (open-source) hard-codes the critical path and leaves pockets of LLM freedom ([Atlas](https://www.salesforce.com/agentforce/what-is-a-reasoning-engine/atlas/); [determinism guide](https://www.salesforce.com/agentforce/levels-of-determinism/); [job-ready release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/stories/agentforce-job-ready-ai-agents/)).
- **Own model:** Koa, a CRM reasoning model built on NVIDIA Nemotron, trained only on synthetic scenarios, weights owned and run by Salesforce (pilot) ([Dreamforce recap](https://www.salesforce.com/blog/dreamforce-2026-announcements/)).
- **Evaluation loop:** Agent Optimizer reads thousands of production sessions and ranks failure patterns by impact; Custom Scorers grade sessions against customer-defined logic; A/B Experimentation splits traffic between agent variants; Agent Health Monitoring has 16+ metrics (same source). Salesforce's CRMArena-Pro benchmark reportedly found agents succeed in about 58% of single-turn and 35% of multi-turn business tasks ([PPC Land](https://ppc.land/salesforces-hunter-agent-gets-demandbase-intent-data-with-no-extra-contract/), third-party), which explains the emphasis on deterministic paths.
- **Trust Layer:** PII masking before prompts leave, grounding only on data the user may see, toxicity checks, zero retention with LLM providers ([developer blog](https://developer.salesforce.com/blogs/2023/10/inside-the-einstein-trust-layer)).
- **Jobs:** an "Agentic Selling" team builds agents for Salesforce's own sellers, developing "agent topics, skills, and conversation flows" for account research, content recommendations and coaching ([Dice listing](https://www.dice.com/job-detail/49ff77fa-1932-4fb5-9fe1-23a3dcde3211), search extract).
- Hunter's ranking model and LLMs used: Not published.

#### 5. Why they get the results they get
- **Data gravity and distribution:** agents sit on the CRM record and are sold into an existing installed base; more than half of Q1 Agentforce and Data 360 bookings came from existing customers ([Q1 release](https://www.salesforce.com/news/press-releases/2026/05/27/fy27-q1-earnings/)).
- **Partner data instead of owned data:** the Data Vendor Gateway turns intent and contact vendors into suppliers, removing a procurement step for buyers.
- **Governance as a buying reason** for large enterprises.
- **Limits:** headline ARR is flattered by the widened definition; agent results are single-customer anecdotes; pricing targets large orgs; Hunter is still a pilot.

#### 6. What we can learn and adopt
- **Next Best Action (NBA) and Next Best Campaign (NBC):** let a user set a goal per segment ("open 5 conversations with water utilities this quarter") and have the NBA engine plan towards it, persisting progress per account across weeks. This matches long industrial cycles better than one-off recommendations.
- **Campaign execution:** encode each play as fixed steps (Agent Script style) and let AI only fill message wording and research, with an approval gate on anything that reaches a customer; log every action.
- **Prospecting:** treat third-party data as plug-in sources behind one interface (CSV today, APIs later), so customers can bring their own intent or contact vendor.
- **Content creation:** let users "teach a skill once" (e.g. "how we write a first email to a plant engineer") and reuse it across segments.
- **Follow-up and response tracking:** store reviewer edits and approvals as labelled data and run simple A/B tests between message variants per segment.
- **Qualified lead identification:** target untouched or stale leads in the customer's own list as a first wedge; the 130,000 leads to 3,200 opportunities format is a simple proof story.

#### 7. What I liked about their approach
_To be completed._

### 23. 6sense (Sales Intelligence and Revenue AI platform)
**Website:** [6sense.com/platform/sales-intelligence](https://6sense.com/platform/sales-intelligence/) | **Founded / HQ:** 2013 / San Francisco | **Category:** predictive account intelligence and ABM platform, now positioned as "the intelligence engine for agentic go-to-market"

**Differentiator:** 6sense trains a qualification model per customer on that customer's own purchase outcomes (the 6QA), attaches the reasons to each score, and publishes a counterfactual comparing opportunities on 6QA accounts with all others. It is also unusually open, through job postings, about how it is building its agent platform (LangGraph, risk-tiered human approval, cost per successful outcome).

**Source coverage:** Website: read ([email agents](https://6sense.com/platform/email-agent/), [manufacturing](https://6sense.com/solutions/manufacturing/)); Sales Intelligence page read in pass 1 ([product page](https://6sense.com/platform/sales-intelligence/)) | Pricing: not published; third-party only ([Landbase](https://www.landbase.com/blog/6sense-pricing)) | Docs: could not access (knowledge base behind RevCity login) | Changelog: search extract only ([Summer 2026 release](https://6sense.com/product-updates/summer-release-2026)) | Blog: titles only (resources on [email agents page](https://6sense.com/platform/email-agent/)) | Case studies: read, headline figures ([email agents page](https://6sense.com/platform/email-agent/)) | Video: titles only (webinars on [manufacturing page](https://6sense.com/solutions/manufacturing/)) | Leadership: read (CPO Kimberly Bloomston quotes in [MCP release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260714566309/en/)) | Press: read ([MCP server](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260714566309/en/), [Summer release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260810228336/en/6sense-Brings-Intelligence-Directly-Into-AI-Agents-and-Entire-GTM-Stack-with-Latest-Product-Releases)) | Jobs: read, 2 postings in full plus board ([Sr. ML Engineer](https://boards.greenhouse.io/6sense/jobs/8064973?gh_jid=8064973), [Principal AI Architect](https://boards.greenhouse.io/6sense/jobs/5636812?gh_jid=5636812), [openings](https://6sense.com/about-us/careers/join-us/)) | Integrations: titles only ([partner library](https://6sense.com/partner-library/?tab=integration)); third-party customer doc read in pass 1 ([GitLab handbook](https://handbook.gitlab.com/handbook/marketing/marketing-operations/6sense/)) | Filings: not applicable (private)

#### 1. Why they are performing well
**Verified facts**
- Passed $200M ARR in fiscal 2024 ([press release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20240327571865/en/6sense-Surpasses-200M-ARR-Driven-by-Sales-Intelligence-Innovation-and-Market-Share-Expansion)); valued at $5.2bn in 2022 ([Bloomberg](https://www.bloombergquint.com/markets/6sense-reaches-5-2-billion-value-with-softbank-joining-round/), third-party). Later revenue: Not published.
- Chris Ball became CEO in September 2025 ([press release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20250909561976/en)); layoffs reported April 2025 ([TrueUp](https://partners.trueup.io/co/6sense), third-party).
- 2026 launches: MCP Server open beta (14 July) for Revenue Marketing customers, GA planned August ([MCP release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260714566309/en/)); Summer release (10 August) with MCP, new APIs (people search, enrichment, keyword intent, account web visits into warehouses and ML pipelines), better people data and executive identification, and ad targeting on recommended contacts ([Summer release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260810228336/en/6sense-Brings-Intelligence-Directly-Into-AI-Agents-and-Entire-GTM-Stack-with-Latest-Product-Releases)).
- RevvyAI (November 2025) is a conversational workspace spanning several agents, with always-on agents for account qualification, ad creative and keywords ([RevvyAI release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20251112256456/en/6sense-Introduces-RevvyAI-Turning-the-Platform-Into-an-AI-Powered-GTM-Command-Center)).
- Analyst recognition: Leader in the 2026 Gartner Magic Quadrant for ABM Platforms for the sixth year (October 2026), Forrester Wave for Revenue Marketing (Q1 2026) and Conversation Automation (Q4 2025) ([newsroom listing](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260714566309/en/); [email agents page](https://6sense.com/platform/email-agent/)).
- Hiring is concentrated in AI and predictive roles: Principal AI Architect (Agentic Platform), Sr. AI Engineer, Sr. ML Engineer, Product Lead (Predictive Platform), Sr. PM for Specialized Modelling and Verticals ([openings](https://6sense.com/about-us/careers/join-us/)).

**Vendor claims**
- Measured by 6sense across its predictive customers (Q2 2025 to Q1 2026): opportunities on 6QA accounts carry 29% higher opportunity values and convert to 33% larger deals than non-6QA opportunities ([MCP release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260714566309/en/)).
- Email agents: Vendavo $3.3M pipeline and "3x return using AI Email vs BDRs"; Simpro 42% reply rate; Harri $12M pipeline in a quarter with a 4% qualified rate; Reltio 1,098 BDR hours saved ([email agents page](https://6sense.com/platform/email-agent/)).
- Auth0 "$3M+ in pipeline in six weeks"; Bynder 250% more outbound pipeline ([product page](https://6sense.com/platform/sales-intelligence/)).

#### 2. How they promise results
- The unit of value is the in-market account at a known buying stage. Proof is a quarterly counterfactual reviewed with customers, which is stronger than most vendors' anecdotes, though it is vendor-measured ([MCP release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260714566309/en/)).
- Scale claims: Signalverse captures "one trillion signals" daily across 200,000+ B2B properties ([job posting](https://boards.greenhouse.io/6sense/jobs/8064973?gh_jid=8064973); [MCP release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260714566309/en/)).
- Pricing is quote-based; third parties report a single Sales Intelligence licence configured with data credits and/or Predictive AI, plus a free tier ([Landbase](https://www.landbase.com/blog/6sense-pricing), third-party). Not outcome-priced.

#### 3. How they generate their outputs
1. **Signals in:** first-party web visits de-anonymised to accounts ("no forms needed"), third-party keyword intent from the Signalverse network, CRM and marketing automation activity, firmographics, technographics and people data ([manufacturing page](https://6sense.com/solutions/manufacturing/); [GitLab handbook](https://handbook.gitlab.com/handbook/marketing/marketing-operations/6sense/), third-party).
2. **Resolve and enrich:** account matching, people and executive identification, CRM matching ([Summer release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260810228336/en/6sense-Brings-Intelligence-Directly-Into-AI-Agents-and-Entire-GTM-Stack-with-Latest-Product-Releases)).
3. **Predict:** per-customer models assign a buying stage (target, awareness, consideration, decision, purchase) and flag a 6QA when an account reaches a late stage; models are "trained on each customer's actual purchase outcomes rather than engagement proxies" and each score ships with "which signals moved it and how it compares to past outcomes" ([MCP release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260714566309/en/); stage definitions from [Saleswhale glossary](https://saleswhale.com/glossary/), third-party, search extract).
4. **Prioritise for sellers:** prioritised account lists, sales alerts by territory, list builder, company and people search, Sales Copilot research ([product page](https://6sense.com/platform/sales-intelligence/)).
5. **Execute:** AI Email Agents build a multi-email sequence from one prompt; marketers upload brand voice and product documents, pick which data points personalise each section, preview, then launch; agents classify replies, answer, follow up and hand off to a rep by rule; sending from 6sense, Gmail or Outlook with contact validation and bounce monitoring ([email agents page](https://6sense.com/platform/email-agent/)). Plays include intent-based outbound, inbound qualification, re-engaging cold prospects and event follow-up.
6. **Expose to other agents:** MCP Server and APIs make 6QA, stage, keyword intent and campaign data callable from Claude, ChatGPT, Writer and Agentforce ([Summer release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260810228336/en/6sense-Brings-Intelligence-Directly-Into-AI-Agents-and-Entire-GTM-Stack-with-Latest-Product-Releases)).
7. **Write back:** scores to Salesforce account, contact and lead fields ([GitLab handbook](https://handbook.gitlab.com/handbook/marketing/marketing-operations/6sense/), third-party).

#### 4. Technical mechanisms and interesting approaches
- **Per-tenant outcome-trained models with explanations:** the most transferable idea in this batch. Features and algorithms: Not published beyond "patented buying-behaviour modelling".
- **Explainability as a product goal:** the ML role is to explain "why an account matters, why now, and who is deciding" with "cited, explainable answers"; models must be "explainable rather than black-box"; stack includes LangGraph, LangChain, Amazon Bedrock, AWS, Databricks, retrieval and multi-agent workflows ([Sr. ML Engineer](https://boards.greenhouse.io/6sense/jobs/8064973?gh_jid=8064973)).
- **Agent platform blueprint (job posting):** LangGraph typed state graphs with checkpointing, durable execution and human-in-the-loop; LangSmith for tracing and evaluation; working, episodic and semantic memory with tenant-scoped context; a typed tool and skill registry; MCP layer with tool-poisoning defences; "human-in-the-loop workflows based on risk tiers"; controls on AI-generated GTM content including "claim substantiation, disclosures, consent, suppression rules"; metrics such as groundedness, citation accuracy and "cost per successful outcome"; model routing across frontier, small and fine-tuned models ([Principal AI Architect](https://boards.greenhouse.io/6sense/jobs/5636812?gh_jid=5636812)). This is the most detailed public view of an incumbent's agent architecture found in this batch.
- **Identity resolution:** reverse IP and cookies ([Contrary Research](https://research.contrary.com/report/6sense), third-party). Match method: Not published.
- **Vertical modelling:** a PM role for "Specialized Modeling & Verticals" ([openings](https://6sense.com/about-us/careers/join-us/)). Inference: industry-specific models are being built.
- LLMs used for email agents and RevvyAI: Not published (Bedrock in the stack suggests multiple hosted models; Inference).

#### 5. Why they get the results they get
- **Network data** (web pixel plus publisher intent) built over 13 years, much of it proprietary, so competitors buying the same feeds cannot replicate it ([MCP release](https://www.businesswire.com/news/home/20260714566309/en/)).
- **A shared unit (6QA)** that marketing and sales both accept, with outcome-based training that adapts to each customer.
- **Coverage of industrial buyers:** manufacturing customers include Viking Pump, Belden, Georgia-Pacific, Hitachi, ChemPoint, Mirion and FARO; positioning covers manufacturers, channel partners and distributors, and claims buying groups average 10 stakeholders (vendor claim) ([manufacturing page](https://6sense.com/solutions/manufacturing/)).
- **Weaknesses:** expensive and complex; account-level intent still needs people data and messaging, which is why email agents and people data were added; growth since 2024 undisclosed.

#### 6. What we can learn and adopt
- **Lead scoring:** fit a simple per-customer model (logistic regression) on the pilot client's closed-won versus closed-lost accounts when enough history exists, and show the top three contributing signals beside each score.
- **Segmentation:** add a buying-stage field per account (awareness, consideration, decision) derived from signal mix and recency, and segment by stage as well as fit.
- **Qualified lead identification:** define one named threshold ("Demand-qualified account") agreed with the client's sales team, and report quarterly how opportunities on qualified accounts compare with the rest, as 6sense does.
- **Prospecting:** for industrial clients, include channel partner and distributor accounts as a separate list type, since 6sense sells this use case to manufacturers.
- **Content creation:** let users upload brand voice and product documents and choose, per email section, which data point personalises it; add a pre-send check that every product claim maps to an approved source.
- **Follow-up and response tracking:** classify replies (interested, objection, not now, wrong person) and route hand-offs by rule, recording who received each.

#### 7. What I liked about their approach
